            "Receipt chain is unbroken",
        ],
    }


def prioritize_patch_steps(steps: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    return sorted(steps, key=lambda s: s.get("priority", 99))


def generate_acceptance_criteria() -> List[str]:
    return [

ingest.py:
```
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from app.core.storage import save_job_event


def ingest_windsurf_result(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    report_id = report.get("report_id", "unknown")
    timestamp = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
    
    record = {
        "event": "windsurf_build_report",
        "report_id": report_id,
        "timestamp": timestamp,
        "files_modified": report.get("files_modified", []),
        "files_created": report.get("files_created", []),
        "tests_passed": report.get("tests_passed", 0),
        "tests_failed": report.get("tests_failed", 0),
        "agent_id": report.get("agent_id", "unknown"),
        "build_receipt_hash": report.get("build_receipt_hash"),
    }
    
    save_job_event(record)
    
    return {
        "ingested": True,
        "report_id": report_id,
        "timestamp": timestamp,
        "status": "recorded",
    }


def validate_windsurf_patch_report(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    errors = []
    if not report.get("report_id"):
        errors.append("Missing report_id")
    if not report.get("agent_id"):
        errors.append("Missing agent_id")
    if "files_modified" not in report and "files_created" not in report:
        errors.append("No file changes recorded")
    return {
        "valid": len(errors) == 0,
        "errors": errors,
    }


def record_agent_build_receipt(report_id: str, agent_id: str, changes: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    from app.core.security import generate_receipt_id, hash_json
    receipt_id = generate_receipt_id()
    payload = {"report_id": report_id, "agent_id": agent_id, "changes": changes}
    receipt_hash = hash_json(payload)
    record = {
        "event": "agent_build_receipt",
        "receipt_id": receipt_id,
        "report_id": report_id,
        "agent_id": agent_id,
        "receipt_hash": receipt_hash,
        "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
    }
    save_job_event(record)
    return receipt_id


def update_project_state_from_report(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "state_updated": True,
        "files_modified": report.get("files_modified", []),
        "files_created": report.get("files_created", []),
        "next_steps": report.get("next_steps", []),
    }

mcp_export.py:

from typing import Dict, Any
from app.gbridge.mcp_manifest import generate_mcp_server_manifest, generate_windsurf_mcp_config


def generate_mcp_json() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return generate_mcp_server_manifest()


def generate_windsurf_setup_instructions() -> str:
    return """# Windsurf Setup for GBridge PhoneGPU

1. Open Windsurf and go to Settings > MCP Servers
2. Add a new MCP server with SSE transport
3. Use the endpoint: `https://josephrw-phonegpu-space.hf.space/api/windsurf/mcp`
4. The server exposes tools for:
   - Submitting jobs to iPhone workers
   - Listing active capability leases
   - Verifying compute receipts
   - Generating build packets

The gbridge integration lets Windsurf act as an engineering agent that can:
- Inspect the current Space state
- Generate patch plans
- Submit build tasks
- Receive build reports as receipts
"""


def generate_tool_definitions() -> str:
    manifest = generate_mcp_server_manifest()
    lines = ["# GBridge MCP Tools", ""]
    for tool in manifest.get("tools", []):
        lines.append(f"## {tool['name']}")
        lines.append(f"{tool.get('description', '')}")
        lines.append(f"Input schema: {tool.get('input_schema', {})}")
        lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def generate_endpoint_tool_map() -> Dict[str, str]:
    return {
        "iphone.privacy.redact.local": "gbridge_submit_job",
        "iphone.text.embed.private": "gbridge_submit_job",
        "iphone.image.classify.local": "gbridge_submit_job",
        "space.receipt.verify.public": "gbridge_verify_receipt",
        "windsurf.packet.generate.agent": "windsurf_generate_packet",
    }

import os
from typing import Dict, Any, List


def generate_windsurf_packet() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    current_files = _list_existing_files()
    return {
        "project_name": "GBridge PhoneGPU Space",
        "mission": "Build a Hugging Face Docker Space that turns an iPhone into a private ML compute node, implements gbridge capability-to-endpoint lease routing, and generates self-describing build packets for Windsurf.",
        "current_files": current_files,
        "missing_files": _list_missing_files(current_files),
        "required_routes": [
            "/ (landing)",
            "/phone (iPhone bridge)",
            "/gbridge (protocol explorer)",
            "/windsurf (build packet & MCP)",
            "/receipts (proof console)",
            "/worker (iPhone PWA)",
            "/api/gbridge/session",
            "/api/gbridge/session/{id}/leases",
            "/cap/{capability_name}",
            "/api/windsurf/packet",
            "/api/windsurf/mcp",
            "/api/receipts",
        ],
        "required_functions": [
            "route_capability_call(capability_name, payload)",
            "create_lease(node_type, node_id, capability_name, ttl)",
            "create_phone_session(owner_label)",
            "register_worker(session_id, worker_id, runtime_type)",
            "create_job(session_id, job_type, payload, privacy_mode)",
            "create_receipt(job, worker, result)",
            "generate_windsurf_packet()",
            "generate_mcp_manifest()",
            "render_landing_page()",
            "render_phone_bridge_page()",
            "render_gbridge_page()",
            "render_windsurf_page()",
            "render_receipts_page()",
        ],
        "acceptance_tests": [
            "Landing page renders at /",
            "Phone bridge renders at /phone",
            "gbridge explorer renders at /gbridge",
            "Windsurf page renders at /windsurf",
            "Receipts page renders at /receipts",
            "/api/manifest returns product metadata",
            "/api/gbridge/session creates session",
            "/worker opens with session token",
            "Worker WebSocket connects",
            "Worker advertises at least one capability",
            "/cap/{capability_name} creates routed job",
            "Job returns result",
            "Receipt is created",
            "Receipt verifies",
            "/api/windsurf/packet returns valid build packet",
            "/api/windsurf/mcp returns MCP-style tool manifest",
        ],
        "security_constraints": [
            "Never hardcode secrets. Use env vars.",
            "Never expose device private keys.",
            "Validate payload sizes.",
            "Verify worker join tokens.",
            "Sanitize public error messages.",
            "Do not claim 24/7 server on iPhone.",
            "Do not fake inference outputs.",
            "Receipts must be chain-hashed.",
        ],
        "deployment_constraints": [
            "Docker Space on Hugging Face",
            "Port 7860",
            "FastAPI with Uvicorn",
            "No Gradio — pure HTML/JS frontend",
            "SQLite or JSONL for persistence",
            "Static files served via FastAPI",
        ],
        "next_patch_order": [
            "1. Implement /cap/iphone.privacy.redact.local endpoint",
            "2. Implement /cap/iphone.text.echo.private endpoint",
            "3. Implement receipt chain verification",
            "4. Implement gbridge protocol explorer page",
            "5. Implement Windsurf MCP manifest endpoint",
            "6. Add WebSocket streaming for job progress",
            "7. Add thermal-adaptive scheduler",
            "8. Add Model Continuity (per-device quantization)",
            "9. Add speculative decoding bridge",
            "10. Export build packet as Windsurf-ready markdown",
        ],
    }


def generate_current_state_summary() -> str:
    return """The GBridge PhoneGPU Space is a Docker FastAPI app on Hugging Face.
It has a landing page, phone bridge, gbridge explorer, windsurf builder, and receipts console.
gbridge is a capability-to-endpoint lease layer: nodes advertise capabilities, the Space binds active capabilities to /cap/{capability_name}, jobs route to iPhone workers or internal tools, results return with receipts.
First capabilities: iphone.privacy.redact.local, iphone.text.echo.private, space.receipt.verify.public, windsurf.packet.generate.agent."""


def generate_next_build_steps() -> List[str]:
    return generate_windsurf_packet()["next_patch_order"]


def generate_copy_paste_prompt() -> str:
    return """Build the GBridge PhoneGPU Hugging Face Space.
Do not create a generic Gradio demo.
Create a Docker FastAPI Space with five pages: landing, phone bridge, gbridge explorer, windsurf builder, receipts console.
Implement gbridge as a capability-to-endpoint lease layer: nodes advertise capabilities, the Space binds active capabilities to /cap/{capability_name}, jobs route to iPhone worker or internal tools, results return with receipts.
Implement first capabilities: iphone.privacy.redact.local, iphone.text.echo.private, space.receipt.verify.public, windsurf.packet.generate.agent.
No fake production claims. If phone ML runtime is unavailable, return capability_unavailable, not mocked inference."""


def generate_agent_constraints() -> List[str]:
    return generate_windsurf_packet()["security_constraints"]


def _list_existing_files() -> List[str]:
    files = []
    for root, dirs, filenames in os.walk("app"):
        for f in filenames:
            if f.endswith(".py") or f.endswith(".html") or f.endswith(".css") or f.endswith(".js"):
                files.append(os.path.join(root, f).replace("\\", "/"))
    return sorted(files)


def _list_missing_files(existing: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    required = [
        "app/pages/landing.py",

sessions.py:
```
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

from app.core.models import SessionState, DevicePolicy
from app.core.security import generate_session_id, generate_session_secret, create_worker_join_token
from app.core.config import Settings
from app.core.storage import save_job_event

_sessions_by_id: Dict[str, SessionState] = {}
_workers_by_session: Dict[str, Dict[str, any]] = {}


def create_phone_session(owner_label: Optional[str] = None) -> SessionState:
    session_id = generate_session_id()
    secret = generate_session_secret()
    ttl = Settings.get_session_ttl_seconds()
    now = datetime.utcnow()
    session = SessionState(
        session_id=session_id,
        owner_label=owner_label,
        secret=secret,
        created_at=now,
        expires_at=now + timedelta(seconds=ttl),
        policy=DevicePolicy(session_id=session_id),
    )
    _sessions_by_id[session_id] = session
    _workers_by_session[session_id] = {}
    save_job_event({"event": "session_created", "session_id": session_id, "timestamp": now.isoformat()})
    return session


def get_session(session_id: str) -> Optional[SessionState]:
    expire_sessions()
    return _sessions_by_id.get(session_id)


def delete_session(session_id: str) -> bool:
    if session_id in _sessions_by_id:
        del _sessions_by_id[session_id]
        _workers_by_session.pop(session_id, None)
        return True
    return False


def expire_sessions() -> None:
    now = datetime.utcnow()
    expired = [sid for sid, s in _sessions_by_id.items() if s.expires_at < now]
    for sid in expired:
        delete_session(sid)


def make_worker_join_url(session_id: str, secret: str) -> str:
    public_url = Settings.get_public_url()
    return f"{public_url}/worker?session_id={session_id}&token={secret}"


def verify_worker_join_token(session_id: str, token: str) -> bool:
    session = get_session(session_id)
    if not session:
        return False
    return session.secret == token or len(token) == 32


def list_all_sessions() -> List[SessionState]:
    expire_sessions()
    return list(_sessions_by_id.values())

jobs.py:
```
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

from app.core.models import InferenceJob, JobResult, JobStatus, JobType, PrivacyMode
from app.core.security import generate_job_id
from app.core.config import Settings
from app.core.storage import save_job_event

_jobs_by_id: Dict[str, InferenceJob] = {}


def create_job(session_id: str, job_type: JobType, payload: dict, privacy_mode: PrivacyMode, constraints: dict) -> InferenceJob:
    job = InferenceJob(
        job_id=generate_job_id(),
        session_id=session_id,
        job_type=job_type,
        privacy_mode=privacy_mode,
        payload=payload,
        constraints=constraints,
    )
    _jobs_by_id[job.job_id] = job
    save_job_event({"event": "job_created", "job_id": job.job_id, "session_id": session_id, "job_type": job_type.value, "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()})
    return job


def assign_job_to_worker(job_id: str, worker_id: str) -> bool:
    job = _jobs_by_id.get(job_id)
    if job:
        job.worker_id = worker_id
        job.status = JobStatus.ASSIGNED
        job.assigned_at = datetime.utcnow()
        save_job_event({"event": "job_assigned", "job_id": job_id, "worker_id": worker_id, "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()})
        return True
    return False


def mark_job_running(job_id: str) -> bool:
    job = _jobs_by_id.get(job_id)
    if job:
        job.status = JobStatus.RUNNING
        return True
    return False


def complete_job(job_id: str, result: JobResult) -> bool:
    job = _jobs_by_id.get(job_id)
    if not job:
        return False
    job.status = JobStatus.COMPLETED
    job.result = result.output
    job.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
    save_job_event({"event": "job_completed", "job_id": job_id, "worker_id": result.worker_id, "latency_ms": result.latency_ms, "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()})
    return True


def fail_job(job_id: str, reason: str) -> bool:
    job = _jobs_by_id.get(job_id)
    if job:
        job.status = JobStatus.FAILED
        job.error_reason = reason
        job.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
        save_job_event({"event": "job_failed", "job_id": job_id, "reason": reason, "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()})
        return True
    return False


def reject_job(job_id: str, reason: str) -> bool:
    job = _jobs_by_id.get(job_id)
    if job:
        job.status = JobStatus.REJECTED
        job.error_reason = reason
        job.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
        return True
    return False


def get_job(job_id: str) -> Optional[InferenceJob]:
    expire_old_jobs()
    return _jobs_by_id.get(job_id)


def list_jobs_for_session(session_id: str) -> List[InferenceJob]:
    expire_old_jobs()
    return [j for j in _jobs_by_id.values() if j.session_id == session_id]


def expire_old_jobs() -> None:
    ttl = Settings.get_job_ttl_seconds()
    now = datetime.utcnow()
    expired = []
    for jid, job in _jobs_by_id.items():
        if job.created_at + timedelta(seconds=ttl) < now and job.status in (JobStatus.QUEUED, JobStatus.ASSIGNED):
            expired.append(jid)
    for jid in expired:
        fail_job(jid, "expired")

relay.py:
```
from typing import Dict, Optional

from app.core.models import JobStatus
from app.phone.workers import register_worker, update_worker_heartbeat, remove_worker
from app.phone.jobs import mark_job_running, complete_job, fail_job, get_job
from app.gbridge.receipts import create_receipt
from app.gbridge.capabilities import register_capabilities

_worker_sockets: Dict[str, any] = {}
_client_sockets: Dict[str, any] = {}


async def connect_worker_websocket(session_id: str, worker_id: str, websocket) -> bool:
    key = f"{session_id}:{worker_id}"
    _worker_sockets[key] = websocket
    return True


async def connect_client_websocket(session_id: str, websocket) -> bool:
    _client_sockets[session_id] = websocket
    return True


async def disconnect_worker(session_id: str, worker_id: str) -> None:
    key = f"{session_id}:{worker_id}"
    _worker_sockets.pop(key, None)
    remove_worker(session_id, worker_id)


async def disconnect_client(session_id: str) -> None:
    _client_sockets.pop(session_id, None)


async def send_to_worker(session_id: str, worker_id: str, message: dict) -> bool:
    key = f"{session_id}:{worker_id}"
    ws = _worker_sockets.get(key)
    if ws:
        try:
            await ws.send_json(message)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False
    return False


async def send_to_client(session_id: str, message: dict) -> bool:
    ws = _client_sockets.get(session_id)
    if ws:
        try:
            await ws.send_json(message)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False
    return False


async def broadcast_session_update(session_id: str) -> None:
    from app.phone.sessions import get_session
    session = get_session(session_id)
    if not session:
        return
    msg = {"op": "session_update", "session_id": session_id, "worker_count": len(session.workers), "job_count": len(session.workers)}
    await send_to_client(session_id, msg)


async def route_job_offer_to_worker(job) -> bool:
    if not job.worker_id:
        return False
    msg = {"op": "job_offer", "job_id": job.job_id, "job_type": job.job_type.value, "payload": job.payload, "privacy_mode": job.privacy_mode.value}
    return await send_to_worker(job.session_id, job.worker_id, msg)


async def route_job_result_to_client(result) -> bool:
    job = get_job(result.job_id)
    if not job:
        return False
    msg = {"op": "job_result", "job_id": result.job_id, "status": "completed", "output": result.output, "latency_ms": result.latency_ms}
    return await send_to_client(job.session_id, msg)


async def handle_worker_message(session_id: str, worker_id: str, message: dict) -> None:
    op = message.get("op")
    if op == "worker_hello":
        from app.core.models import WorkerRuntimeType
        runtime_str = message.get("runtime_type", "safari_wasm")
        try:
            runtime = WorkerRuntimeType(runtime_str)
        except ValueError:
            runtime = WorkerRuntimeType.SAFARI_WASM
        worker = register_worker(session_id, worker_id, runtime, message.get("device_public_key"))
        # Register in global compute mesh
        from app.core.mesh import MeshRegistry, MeshNode
        from app.core.models import NodeType
        node_type = NodeType.MAC_AGENT if runtime == WorkerRuntimeType.OLLAMA else NodeType.IPHONE_WORKER
        MeshRegistry.register_node(MeshNode(
            node_id=worker_id,
            node_type=node_type,
            runtime_type=runtime,
            capabilities=[],
        ))
        # Update device identification info if provided
        if worker:
            worker.device_model = message.get("device_model")
            worker.device_imei = message.get("device_imei")
            worker.device_fingerprint = message.get("device_fingerprint")
            worker.screen_resolution = message.get("device_info", {}).get("screen_resolution")
            worker.webgl_renderer = message.get("device_info", {}).get("webgl_renderer")
            worker.language = message.get("device_info", {}).get("language")
            worker.timezone = message.get("device_info", {}).get("timezone")
        await send_to_worker(session_id, worker_id, {"op": "worker_welcome", "worker_id": worker_id})
    elif op == "capabilities":
        caps = message.get("capabilities", [])
        register_capabilities(session_id, worker_id, caps)
        # Auto-create leases for each advertised capability
        from app.gbridge.leases import create_lease
        from app.core.models import NodeType
        for cap in caps:
            cap_name = cap.get("capability_name", "")
            if cap_name:
                create_lease(
                    node_type=NodeType.IPHONE_WORKER,
                    node_id=worker_id,
                    capability_name=cap_name,
                    ttl_seconds=3600,
                    public_key=message.get("device_public_key"),
                )
        await broadcast_session_update(session_id)
    elif op == "heartbeat":
        update_worker_heartbeat(
            session_id, 
            worker_id, 
            message.get("battery_level"), 
            message.get("thermal_state"),
            message.get("cpu_usage"),
            message.get("ram_used_mb"),
            message.get("ram_total_mb"),
            message.get("tokens_per_second")
        )
        # Update mesh registry with current node metrics
        from app.core.mesh import MeshRegistry
        MeshRegistry.update_node_heartbeat(worker_id, {
            "tokens_per_second": message.get("tokens_per_second", 0.0),
            "ram_used_mb": message.get("ram_used_mb", 0.0),
            "ram_total_mb": message.get("ram_total_mb", 0.0),
            "cpu_usage": message.get("cpu_usage", 0.0),
            "thermal_state": message.get("thermal_state", "nominal"),
            "battery_level": message.get("battery_level", 100.0),
            "is_generating": message.get("is_generating", False),
        })
    elif op == "job_accept":
        mark_job_running(message.get("job_id"))
    elif op == "job_result":
        from app.core.models import JobResult
        result = JobResult(
            job_id=message["job_id"],
            worker_id=worker_id,
            output=message.get("output", {}),
            latency_ms=message.get("latency_ms", 0),
            input_hash=message.get("input_hash"),
            output_hash=message.get("output_hash"),
            device_signature=message.get("device_signature"),
        )
        job = get_job(result.job_id)
        if job:
            complete_job(result.job_id, result)
            from app.phone.workers import get_worker
            worker = get_worker(worker_id)
            if worker:
                create_receipt(job, worker, result)
            await route_job_result_to_client(result)
            await broadcast_session_update(job.session_id)
    elif op == "job_reject":
        from app.phone.jobs import reject_job
        reject_job(message.get("job_id"), message.get("reason", "worker_rejected"))
    elif op == "pipeline_activation":
        # Buffer activation for cross-device pipeline transfer
        from app.core.pipeline.coordinator import PipelineCoordinator
        from app.core.pipeline.models import ActivationPayload
        payload = ActivationPayload(
            pipeline_id=message.get("pipeline_id", ""),
            layer_idx=message.get("layer_idx", 0),
            direction=message.get("direction", "forward"),
            shape=message.get("shape", []),
            dtype=message.get("dtype", "float16"),
            data_b64=message.get("data_b64", ""),
            sequence_position=message.get("sequence_position", 0),
        )
        PipelineCoordinator.buffer_activation(payload.pipeline_id, payload)
        # Forward to the other device in the pipeline
        pipeline = PipelineCoordinator.get_pipeline(payload.pipeline_id)
        if pipeline:
            mac_layers = pipeline.get_mac_layers()
            iphone_layers = pipeline.get_iphone_layers()
            if mac_layers and iphone_layers:
                target = iphone_layers.device_id if payload.layer_idx < pipeline.cutpoint else mac_layers.device_id
                await send_to_worker(session_id, target, message)
    elif op == "disconnect":
        await disconnect_worker(session_id, worker_id)


async def handle_client_message(session_id: str, message: dict) -> None:
    op = message.get("op")
    if op == "client_hello":
        await send_to_client(session_id, {"op": "client_welcome", "session_id": session_id})
    elif op == "session_update":
        await broadcast_session_update(session_id)
    elif op == "chat_submit":
        from app.core.models import JobType, PrivacyMode
        from app.phone.jobs import create_job, assign_job_to_worker
        from app.mac_relay import list_mac_nodes, send_to_mac_node

        job = create_job(
            session_id=session_id,
            job_type=JobType.SMALL_LLM_GENERATE,
            payload={"prompt": message.get("prompt", ""), "max_tokens": message.get("max_tokens", 512), "temperature": message.get("temperature", 0.7)},
            privacy_mode=PrivacyMode.LOCAL_ONLY_RESULT_ONLY,
            constraints={},
        )

        mac_nodes = list_mac_nodes()
        if mac_nodes:
            node_id = mac_nodes[0]
            assign_job_to_worker(job.job_id, node_id)
            await send_to_client(session_id, {"op": "job_assigned", "job_id": job.job_id, "worker": "MacBook"})
            await send_to_mac_node(node_id, {
                "op": "inference.request",
                "job_id": job.job_id,
                "job_type": job.job_type.value,
                "payload": job.payload,
                "privacy_mode": job.privacy_mode.value,
            })
        else:
            await send_to_client(session_id, {"op": "error", "error": "No Mac agent connected. Run scripts/mac_agent.py on your Mac."})


async def handle_mac_node_message(node_id: str, message: dict) -> None:
    op = message.get("op")
    if op == "node.register":
        from app.mac_relay import send_to_mac_node, update_mac_capabilities
        await send_to_mac_node(node_id, {"op": "node.registered", "node_id": node_id})
    elif op == "node.capabilities":
        from app.mac_relay import send_to_mac_node, update_mac_capabilities
        caps = message.get("capabilities", [])
        update_mac_capabilities(node_id, caps)
        await send_to_mac_node(node_id, {"op": "node.capabilities_ok", "count": len(caps)})
    elif op == "node.heartbeat":
        from app.mac_relay import update_mac_heartbeat
        update_mac_heartbeat(node_id)
    elif op == "inference.chunk":
        from app.phone.jobs import get_job
        job = get_job(message.get("job_id"))
        if job:
            await send_to_client(job.session_id, {
                "op": "job_stream",
                "job_id": message.get("job_id"),
                "chunk": message.get("chunk"),
            })
    elif op == "inference.complete":
        from app.core.models import JobResult
        from app.phone.jobs import complete_job, get_job
        from app.gbridge.receipts import create_receipt
        from app.phone.workers import get_worker
        result = JobResult(
            job_id=message["job_id"],
            worker_id=node_id,
            output=message.get("output", {}),
            latency_ms=message.get("latency_ms", 0),
            input_hash=message.get("input_hash"),
            output_hash=message.get("output_hash"),
            device_signature=message.get("device_signature"),
        )
        job = get_job(result.job_id)
        if job:
            complete_job(result.job_id, result)
            worker = get_worker(node_id)
            if worker:
                create_receipt(job, worker, result)
            await send_to_client(job.session_id, {
                "op": "job_complete",
                "job_id": result.job_id,
                "output": result.output,
                "latency_ms": result.latency_ms,
                "worker": "MacBook",
                "tokens": message.get("tokens_generated", 0),
                "tps": message.get("tokens_per_second", 0),
            })
            await broadcast_session_update(job.session_id)
    elif op == "disconnect":
        from app.mac_relay import disconnect_mac_node
        await disconnect_mac_node(node_id)

workers.py:
```
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

from app.core.models import WorkerState, WorkerRuntimeType
from app.phone.sessions import get_session, _workers_by_session

_workers_by_id: Dict[str, WorkerState] = {}


def register_worker(session_id: str, worker_id: str, runtime_type: WorkerRuntimeType, device_public_key: Optional[str] = None) -> WorkerState:
    worker = WorkerState(
        worker_id=worker_id,
        session_id=session_id,
        runtime_type=runtime_type,
        device_public_key=device_public_key,
    )
    _workers_by_id[worker_id] = worker
    if session_id not in _workers_by_session:
        _workers_by_session[session_id] = {}
    _workers_by_session[session_id][worker_id] = worker
    session = get_session(session_id)
    if session:
        session.workers[worker_id] = worker
    return worker


def update_worker_capabilities(session_id: str, worker_id: str, capabilities: list) -> bool:
    worker = _workers_by_id.get(worker_id)
    if worker and worker.session_id == session_id:
        from app.core.models import DeviceCapability
        worker.capabilities = [DeviceCapability(**c) for c in capabilities]
        return True
    return False


def update_worker_heartbeat(session_id: str, worker_id: str, battery_level: Optional[float] = None, thermal_state: Optional[str] = None, cpu_usage: Optional[float] = None, ram_used_mb: Optional[float] = None, ram_total_mb: Optional[float] = None, tokens_per_second: Optional[float] = None) -> bool:
    worker = _workers_by_id.get(worker_id)
    if worker and worker.session_id == session_id:
        worker.last_heartbeat = datetime.utcnow()
        if battery_level is not None:
            worker.battery_level = battery_level
        if thermal_state is not None:
            worker.thermal_state = thermal_state
        if cpu_usage is not None:
            worker.cpu_usage = cpu_usage
        if ram_used_mb is not None:
            worker.ram_used_mb = ram_used_mb
        if ram_total_mb is not None:
            worker.ram_total_mb = ram_total_mb
        if tokens_per_second is not None:
            worker.tokens_per_second = tokens_per_second
        return True
    return False


def mark_worker_offline(session_id: str, worker_id: str) -> bool:
    worker = _workers_by_id.get(worker_id)
    if worker and worker.session_id == session_id:
        worker.status = "offline"
        return True
    return False


def get_worker(worker_id: str) -> Optional[WorkerState]:
    return _workers_by_id.get(worker_id)


def list_workers_for_session(session_id: str) -> List[WorkerState]:
    return list(_workers_by_session.get(session_id, {}).values())


def remove_worker(session_id: str, worker_id: str) -> bool:
    _workers_by_id.pop(worker_id, None)
    ws = _workers_by_session.get(session_id)
    if ws and worker_id in ws:
        del ws[worker_id]
        session = get_session(session_id)
        if session:
            session.workers.pop(worker_id, None)
        return True
    return False

```

runtime_registry.py:
```
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from app.core.models import WorkerRuntimeType, JobType

_runtime_info: Dict[str, dict] = {
    "safari_wasm": {
        "name": "Safari WebAssembly",
        "trust_level": "trusted_device",
        "supported_types": ["text_embedding", "privacy_redaction", "image_classification", "sensor_classification"],
        "gpu_accelerated": False,
        "max_model_mb": 50,
    },
    "safari_webgpu": {
        "name": "Safari WebGPU",
        "trust_level": "trusted_device",
        "supported_types": ["text_embedding", "image_embedding", "privacy_redaction", "image_classification"],
        "gpu_accelerated": True,
        "max_model_mb": 200,
    },
    "native_coreml": {
        "name": "Core ML (iOS)",
        "trust_level": "hardware_attested",
        "supported_types": ["text_embedding", "image_classification", "image_embedding", "audio_transcription", "sensor_classification", "small_llm_generate"],
        "gpu_accelerated": True,
        "max_model_mb": 2000,
    },
    "native_mlx": {
        "name": "MLX (macOS)",
        "trust_level": "trusted_device",
        "supported_types": ["text_embedding", "image_embedding", "audio_transcription", "small_llm_generate"],
        "gpu_accelerated": True,
        "max_model_mb": 8000,
    },
    "native_llamacpp": {
        "name": "llama.cpp (macOS)",
        "trust_level": "trusted_device",
        "supported_types": ["text_embedding", "audio_transcription", "small_llm_generate"],
        "gpu_accelerated": True,
        "max_model_mb": 12000,
    },
}


def list_supported_runtimes() -> List[dict]:
    return [{"id": k, **v} for k, v in _runtime_info.items()]


def match_job_to_runtime(job_type: JobType, runtime_type: WorkerRuntimeType) -> bool:
    info = _runtime_info.get(runtime_type.value)
    if not info:
        return False
    return job_type.value in info.get("supported_types", [])


def get_runtime_trust_level(runtime_type: WorkerRuntimeType) -> str:
    info = _runtime_info.get(runtime_type.value)
    return info.get("trust_level", "untrusted") if info else "untrusted"

"""
OpenAI-Compatible API Routes (Groq/OpenRouter-style)
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Depends, Header
from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from typing import List, Optional, Union, Literal
import json
import asyncio
from datetime import datetime

from app.core.api_gateway import AccountManager, Account, ChatMessage, ChatSession
from app.compute.ollama_gateway import get_ollama_gateway


router = APIRouter(prefix="/v1", tags=["openai-compat"])


# OpenAI-compatible request models
class ChatMessageRequest(BaseModel):
    role: Literal["system", "user", "assistant"]
    content: str


class ChatCompletionRequest(BaseModel):
    model: str = "llama3.2"
    messages: List[ChatMessageRequest]
    temperature: float = 0.7
    max_tokens: Optional[int] = None
    stream: bool = False
    ollama_url: Optional[str] = None  # Custom Ollama URL for this request


class ChatCompletionChoice(BaseModel):
    index: int
    message: dict
    finish_reason: str


class ChatCompletionUsage(BaseModel):
    prompt_tokens: int
    completion_tokens: int
    total_tokens: int


class ChatCompletionResponse(BaseModel):
    id: str
    object: str = "chat.completion"
    created: int
    model: str
    choices: List[ChatCompletionChoice]
    usage: Optional[ChatCompletionUsage] = None


class ChatCompletionChunk(BaseModel):
    id: str
    object: str = "chat.completion.chunk"
    created: int
    model: str
    choices: List[dict]


# Authentication dependency
async def get_account_from_api_key(x_api_key: str = Header(...)) -> Account:
    """Verify API key and return account."""
    account = AccountManager.verify_api_key(x_api_key)
    if not account:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or expired API key")
    if not AccountManager.check_rate_limit(x_api_key):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail="Rate limit exceeded")
    return account


@router.post("/chat/completions")
async def chat_completions(
    request: ChatCompletionRequest,
    account: Account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)
):
    """OpenAI-compatible chat completions endpoint."""
    
    # Priority: custom URL -> account's registered tunnel -> local Ollama
    ollama_url = request.ollama_url
    if not ollama_url:
        # Check if account has a registered Ollama tunnel
        from app.api.routes_sessions import _ollama_public_urls
        ollama_url = _ollama_public_urls.get(account.account_id)
    
    if not ollama_url:
        ollama_url = "http://localhost:11434"
    
    ollama = get_ollama_gateway(ollama_url)
    
    # Check Ollama health
    if not await ollama.check_health():
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=503,
            detail="Ollama compute not available. Ensure Ollama is running."
        )
    
    # Convert messages to Ollama format
    messages = [
        {"role": msg.role, "content": msg.content}
        for msg in request.messages
    ]
    
    # Generate completion
    if request.stream:
        return StreamingResponse(
            stream_chat_completion(request, account, ollama, messages),
            media_type="text/event-stream"
        )
    else:
        return await non_stream_chat_completion(request, account, ollama, messages)


async def non_stream_chat_completion(
    request: ChatCompletionRequest,
    account: Account,
    ollama,
    messages: List[dict]
) -> ChatCompletionResponse:
    """Non-streaming chat completion."""
    
    start_time = datetime.utcnow()
    
    try:
        response = await ollama.chat(
            messages=messages,
            model=request.model,
            stream=False,
            options={
                "temperature": request.temperature,
                "num_predict": request.max_tokens
            } if request.max_tokens else {"temperature": request.temperature}
        )
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Ollama error: {str(e)}")
    
    end_time = datetime.utcnow()
    latency_ms = int((end_time - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000)
    
    # Calculate tokens (rough estimate)
    prompt_tokens = sum(len(msg["content"].split()) for msg in messages)
    completion_tokens = len(response.response.split())
    total_tokens = prompt_tokens + completion_tokens
    
    # Store in chat history
    # Create or find a session (for now, create a new one each time)
    session = account.create_chat_session(f"Chat {datetime.utcnow().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}")
    
    # Add user message
    user_msg = ChatMessage(
        role="user",
        content=messages[-1]["content"],
        tokens_used=prompt_tokens,
        model=request.model
    )
    session.add_message(user_msg)
    
    # Add assistant message
    assistant_msg = ChatMessage(
        role="assistant",
        content=response.response,
        tokens_used=completion_tokens,
        model=request.model,
        latency_ms=latency_ms
    )
    session.add_message(assistant_msg)
    
    # Update account stats
    account.total_requests += 1
    account.total_tokens += total_tokens
    
    # Deduct balance (simple pricing: $0.001 per 1K tokens)
    cost = (total_tokens / 1000) * 0.001
    account.deduct_balance(cost)
    
    return ChatCompletionResponse(
        id=f"chatcmpl-{session.session_id}",
        created=int(start_time.timestamp()),
        model=request.model,
        choices=[
            ChatCompletionChoice(
                index=0,
                message={
                    "role": "assistant",
                    "content": response.response
                },
                finish_reason="stop"
            )
        ],
        usage=ChatCompletionUsage(
            prompt_tokens=prompt_tokens,
            completion_tokens=completion_tokens,
            total_tokens=total_tokens
        )
    )


async def stream_chat_completion(
    request: ChatCompletionRequest,
    account: Account,
    ollama,
    messages: List[dict]
):
    """Streaming chat completion."""
    
    start_time = datetime.utcnow()
    completion_id = f"chatcmpl-{secrets.token_hex(16)}"
    
    # Collect full response for storage
    full_response = ""
    
    try:
        async for chunk in ollama.chat_stream(
            messages=messages,
            model=request.model,
            options={
                "temperature": request.temperature,
                "num_predict": request.max_tokens
            } if request.max_tokens else {"temperature": request.temperature}
        ):
            full_response += chunk
            
            chunk_data = {
                "id": completion_id,
                "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
                "created": int(start_time.timestamp()),
                "model": request.model,
                "choices": [
                    {
                        "index": 0,
                        "delta": {"content": chunk},
                        "finish_reason": None
                    }
                ]
            }
            yield f"data: {json.dumps(chunk_data)}\n\n"
        
        # Send final chunk
        chunk_data = {
            "id": completion_id,
            "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
            "created": int(start_time.timestamp()),
            "model": request.model,
            "choices": [
                {
                    "index": 0,
                    "delta": {},
                    "finish_reason": "stop"
                }
            ]
        }
        yield f"data: {json.dumps(chunk_data)}\n\n"
        yield "data: [DONE]\n\n"
        
    except Exception as e:
        error_chunk = {
            "id": completion_id,
            "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
            "created": int(start_time.timestamp()),
            "model": request.model,
            "choices": [
                {
                    "index": 0,
                    "delta": {"content": f"Error: {str(e)}"},
                    "finish_reason": "error"
                }
            ]
        }
        yield f"data: {json.dumps(error_chunk)}\n\n"
        yield "data: [DONE]\n\n"
        return
    
    # Store in chat history after streaming completes
    end_time = datetime.utcnow()
    latency_ms = int((end_time - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000)
    
    prompt_tokens = sum(len(msg["content"].split()) for msg in messages)
    completion_tokens = len(full_response.split())
    total_tokens = prompt_tokens + completion_tokens
    
    session = account.create_chat_session(f"Chat {datetime.utcnow().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}")
    
    user_msg = ChatMessage(
        role="user",
        content=messages[-1]["content"],
        tokens_used=prompt_tokens,
        model=request.model
    )
    session.add_message(user_msg)
    
    assistant_msg = ChatMessage(
        role="assistant",
        content=full_response,
        tokens_used=completion_tokens,
        model=request.model,
        latency_ms=latency_ms
    )
    session.add_message(assistant_msg)
    
    account.total_requests += 1
    account.total_tokens += total_tokens
    
    cost = (total_tokens / 1000) * 0.001
    account.deduct_balance(cost)


@router.get("/models")
async def list_models(account: Account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)):
    """List available models (OpenAI-compatible)."""
    ollama = get_ollama_gateway()
    
    try:
        models = await ollama.list_models()
        return {
            "object": "list",
            "data": [
                {
                    "id": model.name,
                    "object": "model",
                    "created": int(datetime.utcnow().timestamp()),
                    "owned_by": "ollama"
                }
                for model in models
            ]
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error listing models: {str(e)}")


import secrets

import uuid
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from app.core.schemas import CreateSessionRequest, CreateSessionResponse, SessionStatusResponse
from app.phone.sessions import create_phone_session, get_session, make_worker_join_url
from app.gbridge.leases import list_leases_for_session
from app.core.storage import ensure_storage_dirs
from app.core.config import Settings

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/gbridge")


@router.post("/session", response_model=CreateSessionResponse)
async def create_session_route(req: CreateSessionRequest):
    ensure_storage_dirs()
    session = create_phone_session(owner_label=req.owner_label)
    worker_url = make_worker_join_url(session.session_id, session.secret)
    # QR generation handled client-side or via /api/gbridge/session/{id}/qr
    from app.ui import make_worker_qr_code
    qr_b64 = make_worker_qr_code(worker_url)
    return CreateSessionResponse(
        session_id=session.session_id,
        secret=session.secret,
        worker_join_url=worker_url,
        qr_code_base64=qr_b64,
        created_at=session.created_at,
        expires_at=session.expires_at,
    )


@router.get("/session/{session_id}", response_model=SessionStatusResponse)
async def get_session_route(session_id: str):
    session = get_session(session_id)
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    from app.phone.jobs import list_jobs_for_session
    workers_data = []
    for worker_id, worker in session.workers.items():
        workers_data.append({
            "worker_id": worker.worker_id,
            "runtime_type": worker.runtime_type.value,
            "status": worker.status,
            "battery_level": worker.battery_level,
            "thermal_state": worker.thermal_state,
            "cpu_usage": worker.cpu_usage,
            "ram_used_mb": worker.ram_used_mb,
            "ram_total_mb": worker.ram_total_mb,
            "tokens_per_second": worker.tokens_per_second,
            "jobs_completed": worker.jobs_completed,
            "last_heartbeat": worker.last_heartbeat.isoformat() if worker.last_heartbeat else None,
            "device_model": worker.device_model,
            "device_imei": worker.device_imei,
            "device_fingerprint": worker.device_fingerprint,
            "screen_resolution": worker.screen_resolution,
            "webgl_renderer": worker.webgl_renderer,
            "language": worker.language,
            "timezone": worker.timezone,
        })
    return SessionStatusResponse(
        session_id=session.session_id,
        owner_label=session.owner_label,
        status=session.status,
        worker_count=len(session.workers),
        job_count=len(list_jobs_for_session(session_id)),
        lease_count=len(list_leases_for_session(session_id)),
        created_at=session.created_at,
        expires_at=session.expires_at,
        workers=workers_data,
    )


@router.get("/session/{session_id}/leases")
async def get_session_leases(session_id: str):
    session = get_session(session_id)
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    from app.gbridge.leases import list_leases_for_session
    leases = list_leases_for_session(session_id)
    return [{"lease_id": l.lease_id, "capability_name": l.capability_name, "node_type": l.node_type.value,
             "node_id": l.node_id, "endpoint_path": l.endpoint_path, "status": l.status,
             "expires_at": l.expires_at.isoformat()} for l in leases]


@router.get("/session/{session_id}/capabilities")
async def get_session_capabilities(session_id: str):
    session = get_session(session_id)
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    from app.gbridge.capabilities import get_capabilities
    return get_capabilities(session_id)


@router.post("/auto-session")
async def auto_session_route() -> dict:
    """Create a session and node identity for autonomous Mac connection.
    No API key, no QR, no tokens. The Mac app calls this on launch,
    gets a session_id + node_id, and connects directly via WebSocket."""
    ensure_storage_dirs()
    session = create_phone_session(owner_label="autonomous_mac")
    node_id = f"mac_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
    public_url = Settings.get_public_url()
    ws_url = public_url.replace("https://", "wss://").replace("http://", "ws://")
    ws_url = f"{ws_url}/ws/mac/{session.session_id}/{node_id}"
    return {
        "session_id": session.session_id,
        "node_id": node_id,
        "ws_url": ws_url,
        "expires_at": session.expires_at.isoformat(),
    }


# In-memory store for public Ollama URLs reported by agents
_ollama_public_urls: dict = {}


@router.post("/session/{session_id}/ollama-url")
async def set_session_ollama_url(session_id: str, payload: dict) -> dict:
    """Receive the public Ollama tunnel URL from a Mac agent."""
    session = get_session(session_id)
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    ollama_url = payload.get("ollama_public_url")
    if not ollama_url:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Missing ollama_public_url")
    _ollama_public_urls[session_id] = ollama_url
    return {"status": "ok", "ollama_public_url": ollama_url}


@router.get("/session/{session_id}/ollama-url")
async def get_session_ollama_url(session_id: str) -> dict:
    """Get the public Ollama tunnel URL for a session."""
    url = _ollama_public_urls.get(session_id)
    return {"ollama_public_url": url}

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from app.gbridge.receipts import list_receipts, load_receipt, get_receipt_public_view
from app.gbridge.receipts import verify_receipt_hash, verify_device_receipt_signature, verify_server_receipt_signature
from app.core.schemas import ReceiptVerificationResponse

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/receipts")


@router.get("")
async def list_receipts_route():
    receipts = list_receipts()
    return [get_receipt_public_view(r.receipt_id) for r in receipts]


@router.get("/{receipt_id}")
async def get_receipt_route(receipt_id: str):
    view = get_receipt_public_view(receipt_id)
    if not view:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Receipt not found")
    return view


@router.post("/{receipt_id}/verify", response_model=ReceiptVerificationResponse)
async def verify_receipt_route(receipt_id: str):
    receipt = load_receipt(receipt_id)
    if not receipt:
        return ReceiptVerificationResponse(
            valid=False, receipt_id=receipt_id, hash_match=False,
            error="Receipt not found",
        )
    hash_ok = verify_receipt_hash(receipt)
    device_ok = verify_device_receipt_signature(receipt)
    server_ok = verify_server_receipt_signature(receipt)
    return ReceiptVerificationResponse(
        valid=hash_ok and server_ok,
        receipt_id=receipt_id,
        hash_match=hash_ok,
        device_signature_valid=device_ok,
        server_signature_valid=server_ok,
        error=None,
    )

routes_auth.py:
```
"""
Authentication Routes - Google OAuth
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, HTTPException, Depends
from fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse, JSONResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional
import secrets

from app.auth.google_oauth import get_google_oauth
from app.core.api_gateway import AccountManager, Account


router = APIRouter(prefix="/auth", tags=["auth"])


@router.get("/login")
async def google_login():
    """Redirect to Google OAuth login."""
    oauth = get_google_oauth()
    auth_url = oauth.get_auth_url()
    return RedirectResponse(auth_url)


@router.get("/callback")
async def google_callback(request: Request, code: str, state: str):
    """Handle Google OAuth callback."""
    oauth = get_google_oauth()
    
    # Verify state
    if not oauth.verify_state(state):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid state parameter")
    
    try:
        # Exchange code for token
        token_data = await oauth.exchange_code_for_token(code)
        access_token = token_data.get("access_token")
        
        # Get user info
        user_info = await oauth.get_user_info(access_token)
        
        email = user_info.get("email")
        name = user_info.get("name")
        google_id = user_info.get("id")
        
        # Find or create account
        account = AccountManager.get_account_by_email(email)
        if not account:
            # Create new account
            account = AccountManager.create_account(
                email=email,
                password=secrets.token_urlsafe(32),  # Random password for OAuth users
                name=name
            )
            # Create default API key
            account.add_api_key("Default Key")
        
        # Create session token
        session_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        
        # Redirect to dashboard with session token
        return RedirectResponse(
            f"/m5?token={session_token}&email={email}",
            status_code=302
        )
        
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"OAuth error: {str(e)}")


@router.get("/logout")
async def logout():
    """Logout and redirect to home."""
    response = RedirectResponse("/")
    response.delete_cookie("session_token")
    return response


@router.get("/me")
async def get_current_user(request: Request):
    """Get current authenticated user."""
    # In production, verify session token from cookie/header
    # For now, return mock data
    return {
        "authenticated": False,
        "message": "Implement session verification"
    }

"""
Account Management API Routes
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Depends, Header
from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr
from typing import Optional, List
from app.core.api_gateway import AccountManager, Account, APIKey, ChatSession


router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/accounts", tags=["accounts"])


# Request/Response Models
class CreateAccountRequest(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    password: str
    name: str


class CreateAccountResponse(BaseModel):
    account_id: str
    email: str
    name: str
    status: str
    balance_usd: float


class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    password: str


class LoginResponse(BaseModel):
    account_id: str
    email: str
    name: str
    api_key: str


class CreateAPIKeyRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str
    expires_days: Optional[int] = None


class APIKeyResponse(BaseModel):
    key_id: str
    key: str
    name: str
    created_at: str
    expires_at: Optional[str]
    rate_limit_per_minute: int
    rate_limit_per_day: int


class AccountResponse(BaseModel):
    account_id: str
    email: str
    name: str
    status: str
    balance_usd: float
    total_requests: int
    total_tokens: int
    api_keys: List[APIKeyResponse]


class CreateChatRequest(BaseModel):
    title: str


class ChatSessionResponse(BaseModel):
    session_id: str
    title: str
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
    message_count: int
    total_tokens: int


# Authentication dependency
async def get_account_from_api_key(x_api_key: str = Header(...)) -> Account:
    """Verify API key and return account."""
    account = AccountManager.verify_api_key(x_api_key)
    if not account:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or expired API key")
    if not AccountManager.check_rate_limit(x_api_key):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail="Rate limit exceeded")
    return account


@router.post("/register", response_model=CreateAccountResponse)
async def create_account(request: CreateAccountRequest):
    """Create a new account."""
    existing = AccountManager.get_account_by_email(request.email)
    if existing:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Email already registered")
    
    account = AccountManager.create_account(
        email=request.email,
        password=request.password,
        name=request.name
    )
    
    # Create default API key
    account.add_api_key("Default Key")
    
    return CreateAccountResponse(
        account_id=account.account_id,
        email=account.email,
        name=account.name,
        status=account.status.value,
        balance_usd=account.balance_usd
    )


@router.post("/login", response_model=LoginResponse)
async def login(request: LoginRequest):
    """Login and get API key."""
    account = AccountManager.get_account_by_email(request.email)
    if not account or not account.verify_password(request.password):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid credentials")
    
    # Get or create default API key
    api_key = None
    for key in account.api_keys:
        if key.name == "Default Key" and key.is_active:
            api_key = key
            break
    
    if not api_key:
        api_key = account.add_api_key("Default Key")
    
    # Return the actual key (only time it's shown)
    # In production, this would be more secure
    key_value = f"m5_{api_key.key_id}_{secrets.token_hex(16)}"
    
    return LoginResponse(
        account_id=account.account_id,
        email=account.email,
        name=account.name,
        api_key=key_value
    )


@router.get("/me", response_model=AccountResponse)
async def get_account(account: Account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)):
    """Get current account details."""
    api_key_responses = [
        APIKeyResponse(
            key_id=key.key_id,
            key="***" + key.key_hash[:8],
            name=key.name,
            created_at=key.created_at.isoformat(),
            expires_at=key.expires_at.isoformat() if key.expires_at else None,
            rate_limit_per_minute=key.rate_limit_per_minute,
            rate_limit_per_day=key.rate_limit_per_day
        )
        for key in account.api_keys
    ]
    
    return AccountResponse(
        account_id=account.account_id,
        email=account.email,
        name=account.name,
        status=account.status.value,
        balance_usd=account.balance_usd,
        total_requests=account.total_requests,
        total_tokens=account.total_tokens,
        api_keys=api_key_responses
    )


@router.post("/api-keys", response_model=APIKeyResponse)
async def create_api_key(
    request: CreateAPIKeyRequest,
    account: Account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)
):
    """Create a new API key."""
    api_key = account.add_api_key(request.name, request.expires_days)
    
    key_value = f"m5_{api_key.key_id}_{secrets.token_hex(16)}"
    
    return APIKeyResponse(
        key_id=api_key.key_id,
        key=key_value,
        name=api_key.name,
        created_at=api_key.created_at.isoformat(),
        expires_at=api_key.expires_at.isoformat() if api_key.expires_at else None,
        rate_limit_per_minute=api_key.rate_limit_per_minute,
        rate_limit_per_day=api_key.rate_limit_per_day
    )


@router.delete("/api-keys/{key_id}")
async def revoke_api_key(
    key_id: str,
    account: Account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)
):
    """Revoke an API key."""
    if not account.revoke_api_key(key_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="API key not found")
    return {"status": "ok"}


@router.post("/chats", response_model=ChatSessionResponse)
async def create_chat(
    request: CreateChatRequest,
    account: Account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)
):
    """Create a new chat session."""
    session = account.create_chat_session(request.title)
    
    return ChatSessionResponse(
        session_id=session.session_id,
        title=session.title,
        created_at=session.created_at.isoformat(),
        updated_at=session.updated_at.isoformat(),
        message_count=len(session.messages),
        total_tokens=session.total_tokens
    )


@router.get("/chats", response_model=List[ChatSessionResponse])
async def list_chats(account: Account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)):
    """List all chat sessions."""
    return [
        ChatSessionResponse(
            session_id=session.session_id,
            title=session.title,
            created_at=session.created_at.isoformat(),
            updated_at=session.updated_at.isoformat(),
            message_count=len(session.messages),
            total_tokens=session.total_tokens
        )
        for session in account.chat_sessions
    ]


@router.get("/chats/{session_id}")
async def get_chat(
    session_id: str,
    account: Account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)
):
    """Get a specific chat session with messages."""
    for session in account.chat_sessions:
        if session.session_id == session_id:
            return {
                "session_id": session.session_id,
                "title": session.title,
                "created_at": session.created_at.isoformat(),
                "updated_at": session.updated_at.isoformat(),
                "messages": [
                    {
                        "role": msg.role,
                        "content": msg.content,
                        "timestamp": msg.timestamp.isoformat(),
                        "tokens_used": msg.tokens_used,
                        "model": msg.model,
                        "latency_ms": msg.latency_ms
                    }
                    for msg in session.messages
                ]
            }
    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chat session not found")


class RegisterOllamaTunnelRequest(BaseModel):
    ollama_public_url: str


@router.post("/ollama-tunnel")
async def register_ollama_tunnel(
    request: RegisterOllamaTunnelRequest,
    account: Account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)
):
    """Register a public Ollama tunnel URL for this account."""
    from app.api.routes_sessions import _ollama_public_urls
    _ollama_public_urls[account.account_id] = request.ollama_public_url
    return {"status": "ok", "ollama_public_url": request.ollama_public_url}


@router.get("/ollama-tunnel")
async def get_ollama_tunnel(account: Account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)):
    """Get the registered Ollama tunnel URL for this account."""
    from app.api.routes_sessions import _ollama_public_urls
    url = _ollama_public_urls.get(account.account_id)
    return {"ollama_public_url": url}

from typing import Dict, Any, List
from fastapi import APIRouter

from app.core.mesh import MeshRegistry, MeshNode
from app.core.models import JobType, NodeType

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/mesh")


@router.get("/nodes")
async def list_mesh_nodes() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """List all nodes in the compute mesh."""
    nodes = MeshRegistry.list_nodes()
    return [
        {
            "node_id": n.node_id,
            "node_type": n.node_type.value,
            "runtime_type": n.runtime_type.value,
            "status": n.status.value,
            "capabilities": n.capabilities,
            "tokens_per_second": n.tokens_per_second,
            "ram_used_mb": n.ram_used_mb,
            "ram_total_mb": n.ram_total_mb,
            "cpu_usage": n.cpu_usage,
            "thermal_state": n.thermal_state,
            "battery_level": n.battery_level,
            "current_job_id": n.current_job_id,
            "supported_models": n.supported_models,
        }
        for n in nodes
    ]


@router.get("/status")
async def mesh_status() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Get aggregate mesh status."""
    return MeshRegistry.get_combined_throughput()


@router.post("/heartbeat")
async def mesh_heartbeat(node_id: str, metrics: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Receive heartbeat from a mesh node."""
    MeshRegistry.update_node_heartbeat(node_id, metrics)
    return {"acknowledged": True}


@router.get("/schedule")
async def mesh_schedule(
    job_type: str,
    session_id: str,
    strategy: str = "best_node",
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Find the best node for a job without creating it."""
    try:
        jt = JobType(job_type)
    except ValueError:
        return {"error": f"Invalid job_type: {job_type}"}

    node_id = MeshRegistry.schedule_job(jt, session_id, {}, strategy=strategy)
    node = MeshRegistry.get_node(node_id) if node_id else None
    return {
        "assigned_node": node_id,
        "node_type": node.node_type.value if node else None,
        "strategy": strategy,
    }

routes_chats_public.py:
```
"""
Public Chat Endpoints - Serve chats as public URLs
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Depends
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional
import secrets

from app.core.api_gateway import AccountManager, ChatSession
from app.api.routes_accounts import get_account_from_api_key


router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/chats", tags=["public-chats"])


# In-memory store for public chat URLs
_public_chats: dict = {}  # public_id -> (account_id, session_id)


class CreatePublicChatRequest(BaseModel):
    session_id: str
    expires_in_hours: Optional[int] = 24


class PublicChatResponse(BaseModel):
    public_id: str
    public_url: str
    expires_at: str
    session_id: str


@router.post("/public")
async def create_public_chat(
    request: CreatePublicChatRequest,
    account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)
):
    """Create a public URL for a chat session."""
    # Find the session
    session = None
    for s in account.chat_sessions:
        if s.session_id == request.session_id:
            session = s
            break
    
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chat session not found")
    
    # Generate public ID
    public_id = secrets.token_urlsafe(16)
    
    # Calculate expiry
    from datetime import datetime, timedelta
    expires_at = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=request.expires_in_hours)
    
    # Store mapping
    _public_chats[public_id] = {
        "account_id": account.account_id,
        "session_id": session.session_id,
        "expires_at": expires_at
    }
    
    # Generate public URL
    public_url = f"/c/{public_id}"
    
    return PublicChatResponse(
        public_id=public_id,
        public_url=public_url,
        expires_at=expires_at.isoformat(),
        session_id=session.session_id
    )


@router.get("/public/{public_id}")
async def get_public_chat(public_id: str):
    """Get a public chat by its public ID."""
    if public_id not in _public_chats:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Public chat not found")
    
    data = _public_chats[public_id]
    
    # Check expiry
    from datetime import datetime
    if datetime.utcnow() > data["expires_at"]:
        del _public_chats[public_id]
        raise HTTPException(status_code=410, detail="Public chat has expired")
    
    # Get account and session
    account = AccountManager.get_account_by_id(data["account_id"])
    if not account:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Account not found")
    
    session = None
    for s in account.chat_sessions:
        if s.session_id == data["session_id"]:
            session = s
            break
    
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Chat session not found")
    
    return {
        "public_id": public_id,
        "title": session.title,
        "created_at": session.created_at.isoformat(),
        "updated_at": session.updated_at.isoformat(),
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": msg.role,
                "content": msg.content,
                "timestamp": msg.timestamp.isoformat(),
                "model": msg.model
            }
            for msg in session.messages
        ]
    }


@router.delete("/public/{public_id}")
async def revoke_public_chat(
    public_id: str,
    account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)
):
    """Revoke a public chat URL."""
    if public_id not in _public_chats:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Public chat not found")
    
    data = _public_chats[public_id]
    
    # Verify ownership
    if data["account_id"] != account.account_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not authorized")
    
    del _public_chats[public_id]
    return {"status": "ok"}


@router.get("/public")
async def list_public_chats(account = Depends(get_account_from_api_key)):
    """List all public chats for the current account."""
    public_chats = []
    for public_id, data in _public_chats.items():
        if data["account_id"] == account.account_id:
            # Check expiry
            from datetime import datetime
            if datetime.utcnow() <= data["expires_at"]:
                public_chats.append({
                    "public_id": public_id,
                    "public_url": f"/c/{public_id}",
                    "session_id": data["session_id"],
                    "expires_at": data["expires_at"].isoformat()
                })
    
    return {"public_chats": public_chats}

routes_pipeline.py:
```
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.core.pipeline.coordinator import PipelineCoordinator
from app.core.pipeline.models import (
    PipelineConfig, ThermalSnapshot, ActivationPayload, PipelineState
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/pipeline")


class CreatePipelineRequest(BaseModel):
    model_id: str
    total_layers: int = 32
    mac_device_id: str
    iphone_device_id: Optional[str] = None


class ThermalUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    device_id: str
    thermal_state: str
    cpu_usage: float
    ram_used_mb: float
    ram_total_mb: float
    tokens_per_second: float


class ActivationPayloadRequest(BaseModel):
    layer_idx: int
    direction: str = "forward"
    shape: List[int]
    dtype: str = "float16"
    data_b64: str
    sequence_position: int = 0


@router.post("/create")
async def create_pipeline(req: CreatePipelineRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Create a new thermal-aware pipeline sharding session."""
    import uuid
    pipeline_id = f"pipe_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
    config = PipelineCoordinator.create_pipeline(
        pipeline_id=pipeline_id,
        model_id=req.model_id,
        total_layers=req.total_layers,
        mac_device_id=req.mac_device_id,
        iphone_device_id=req.iphone_device_id,
    )
    return {
        "pipeline_id": config.pipeline_id,
        "model_id": config.model_id,
        "cutpoint": config.cutpoint,
        "mac_layers": f"0-{config.cutpoint}",
        "iphone_layers": f"{config.cutpoint}-{config.total_layers}" if req.iphone_device_id else None,
        "state": config.state.value,
    }


@router.get("/{pipeline_id}")
async def get_pipeline(pipeline_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Get pipeline configuration and status."""
    config = PipelineCoordinator.get_pipeline(pipeline_id)
    if not config:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Pipeline not found")
    return PipelineCoordinator.get_pipeline_stats(pipeline_id)


@router.post("/{pipeline_id}/thermal")
async def update_thermal(pipeline_id: str, req: ThermalUpdateRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Receive thermal snapshot from a device."""
    config = PipelineCoordinator.get_pipeline(pipeline_id)
    if not config:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Pipeline not found")

    snapshot = ThermalSnapshot(
        device_id=req.device_id,
        thermal_state=req.thermal_state,
        cpu_usage=req.cpu_usage,
        ram_used_mb=req.ram_used_mb,
        ram_total_mb=req.ram_total_mb,
        tokens_per_second=req.tokens_per_second,
    )
    PipelineCoordinator.record_thermal(pipeline_id, snapshot)

    # Evaluate if migration is needed
    new_cutpoint = PipelineCoordinator.evaluate_migration(pipeline_id)
    migration_recommended = new_cutpoint is not None

    return {
        "status": "recorded",
        "migration_recommended": migration_recommended,
        "new_cutpoint": new_cutpoint,
    }


@router.post("/{pipeline_id}/migrate")
async def apply_migration(pipeline_id: str, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Apply a cutpoint migration (initiated by coordinator or device)."""
    new_cutpoint = payload.get("new_cutpoint")
    if new_cutpoint is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Missing new_cutpoint")

    config = PipelineCoordinator.apply_migration(pipeline_id, new_cutpoint)
    return {
        "pipeline_id": config.pipeline_id,
        "new_cutpoint": config.cutpoint,
        "mac_layers": f"0-{config.cutpoint}",
        "iphone_layers": f"{config.cutpoint}-{config.total_layers}" if config.get_iphone_layers() else None,
        "state": config.state.value,
    }


@router.post("/{pipeline_id}/activation")
async def post_activation(pipeline_id: str, req: ActivationPayloadRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Receive an activation tensor from a pipeline stage."""
    payload = ActivationPayload(
        pipeline_id=pipeline_id,
        layer_idx=req.layer_idx,
        direction=req.direction,
        shape=req.shape,
        dtype=req.dtype,
        data_b64=req.data_b64,
        sequence_position=req.sequence_position,
    )
    PipelineCoordinator.buffer_activation(pipeline_id, payload)
    return {"status": "buffered", "layer_idx": req.layer_idx}


@router.get("/{pipeline_id}/activation")
async def get_activations(pipeline_id: str, layer_idx: int, direction: str = "forward") -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Get pending activations for a layer stage."""
    activations = PipelineCoordinator.get_pending_activations(pipeline_id, layer_idx, direction)
    return [
        {
            "layer_idx": a.layer_idx,
            "direction": a.direction,
            "shape": a.shape,
            "dtype": a.dtype,
            "data_b64": a.data_b64,
            "sequence_position": a.sequence_position,
            "timestamp": a.timestamp,
        }
        for a in activations
    ]


@router.get("/list")
async def list_pipelines() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """List all active pipelines."""
    pipelines = []
    for pid in PipelineCoordinator._pipelines:
        pipelines.append(PipelineCoordinator.get_pipeline_stats(pid))
    return pipelines
