routes_chat.py:
```
import os
import json
from typing import AsyncGenerator, Dict, Any, Optional, List
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel
import httpx

from app.gbridge.mcp_manifest import generate_mcp_server_manifest

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/chat")

HF_API_URL = os.getenv("HF_INFERENCE_URL", "https://api-inference.huggingface.co/models/")
HF_API_TOKEN = os.getenv("HF_API_TOKEN", "")
DEFAULT_MODEL = os.getenv("HF_CHAT_MODEL", "meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct")
OLLAMA_URL = os.getenv("OLLAMA_URL", "http://localhost:11434")


def _discover_public_ollama_url() -> Optional[str]:
    """Discover any public Ollama tunnel URL reported by connected Mac agents."""
    from app.api.routes_sessions import _ollama_public_urls
    for url in _ollama_public_urls.values():
        if url:
            return url
    return None


class ChatMessage(BaseModel):
    role: str
    content: str


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    messages: List[ChatMessage]
    model: Optional[str] = None
    temperature: float = 0.7
    max_tokens: int = 1024
    stream: bool = False
    use_ollama: bool = False


class MCPInvokeRequest(BaseModel):
    tool_name: str
    arguments: Dict[str, Any]


def _build_hf_payload(request: ChatRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    model = request.model or DEFAULT_MODEL
    return {
        "model": model,
        "messages": [{"role": m.role, "content": m.content} for m in request.messages],
        "temperature": request.temperature,
        "max_tokens": request.max_tokens,
        "stream": request.stream,
    }


def _build_ollama_payload(request: ChatRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    model = request.model or "llama3"
    return {
        "model": model,
        "messages": [{"role": m.role, "content": m.content} for m in request.messages],
        "stream": request.stream,
        "options": {
            "temperature": request.temperature,
            "num_predict": request.max_tokens,
        },
    }


async def _stream_hf_chat(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> AsyncGenerator[str, None]:
    model = payload.get("model", DEFAULT_MODEL)
    url = f"{HF_API_URL}{model}/v1/chat/completions"
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    if HF_API_TOKEN:
        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {HF_API_TOKEN}"

    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=120.0) as client:
        async with client.stream("POST", url, json=payload, headers=headers) as response:
            if response.status_code != 200:
                body = await response.aread()
                yield f"data: {json.dumps({'error': f'HF API error {response.status_code}: {body.decode()}'})}\n\n"
                return

            async for line in response.aiter_lines():
                if line.startswith("data: "):
                    data = line[6:]
                    if data == "[DONE]":
                        yield f"data: {json.dumps({'done': True})}\n\n"
                        break
                    try:
                        parsed = json.loads(data)
                        delta = parsed.get("choices", [{}])[0].get("delta", {})
                        content = delta.get("content", "")
                        if content:
                            yield f"data: {json.dumps({'content': content})}\n\n"
                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
                        continue


async def _stream_ollama_chat(payload: Dict[str, Any], ollama_base_url: Optional[str] = None) -> AsyncGenerator[str, None]:
    base = (ollama_base_url or OLLAMA_URL).rstrip("/")
    url = f"{base}/api/chat"
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}

    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=120.0) as client:
        async with client.stream("POST", url, json=payload, headers=headers) as response:
            if response.status_code != 200:
                body = await response.aread()
                yield f"data: {json.dumps({'error': f'Ollama error {response.status_code}: {body.decode()}'})}\n\n"
                return

            async for line in response.aiter_lines():
                if not line:
                    continue
                try:
                    parsed = json.loads(line)
                    content = parsed.get("message", {}).get("content", "")
                    done = parsed.get("done", False)
                    if content:
                        yield f"data: {json.dumps({'content': content})}\n\n"
                    if done:
                        yield f"data: {json.dumps({'done': True})}\n\n"
                        break
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    continue


async def _chat_nonstream_hf(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    model = payload.get("model", DEFAULT_MODEL)
    url = f"{HF_API_URL}{model}/v1/chat/completions"
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    if HF_API_TOKEN:
        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {HF_API_TOKEN}"

    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=120.0) as client:
        response = await client.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
        if response.status_code != 200:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=response.status_code, detail=response.text)
        return response.json()


async def _chat_nonstream_ollama(payload: Dict[str, Any], ollama_base_url: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    base = (ollama_base_url or OLLAMA_URL).rstrip("/")
    url = f"{base}/api/chat"
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}

    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=120.0) as client:
        response = await client.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
        if response.status_code != 200:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=response.status_code, detail=response.text)
        return response.json()


@router.post("/completions")
async def chat_completions(request: ChatRequest):
    # First, try to route via mesh to an available compute node
    from app.core.mesh import MeshRegistry
    from app.core.models import JobType
    from app.gbridge.router import route_capability_call

    mesh_node_id = MeshRegistry.schedule_job(
        JobType.SMALL_LLM_GENERATE,
        session_id="chat_global",
        payload={},
        strategy="best_node"
    )

    if mesh_node_id and not request.use_ollama:
        # Route to mesh node via gbridge capability
        node = MeshRegistry.get_node(mesh_node_id)
        if node and node.node_type.value == "mac_agent":
            # Mac agent with Ollama — route via capability
            payload = {
                "session_id": "chat_global",
                "payload": {
                    "messages": [{"role": m.role, "content": m.content} for m in request.messages],
                    "max_tokens": request.max_tokens,
                    "temperature": request.temperature,
                },
                "job_type": "small_llm_generate",
                "privacy_mode": "local_only_result_only",
            }
            result = route_capability_call(
                "iphone.text.echo.private",
                payload["payload"],
                payload["session_id"]
            )
            # If mesh routing succeeds, return it with mesh metadata
            if result and "error" not in result:
                return {
                    "routed_via_mesh": True,
                    "node_id": mesh_node_id,
                    "node_type": node.node_type.value,
                    **result,
                }

    # Fallback: direct inference
    # Discover if any Mac agent has exposed Ollama via public tunnel
    public_ollama = _discover_public_ollama_url()
    if public_ollama and not request.use_ollama:
        payload = _build_ollama_payload(request)
        if request.stream:
            return StreamingResponse(
                _stream_ollama_chat(payload, ollama_base_url=public_ollama),
                media_type="text/event-stream",
                headers={"X-Ollama-Source": "public-agent-tunnel"},
            )
        return await _chat_nonstream_ollama(payload, ollama_base_url=public_ollama)

    if request.use_ollama:
        payload = _build_ollama_payload(request)
        if request.stream:
            return StreamingResponse(
                _stream_ollama_chat(payload),
                media_type="text/event-stream",
            )
        return await _chat_nonstream_ollama(payload)
    else:
        payload = _build_hf_payload(request)
        if request.stream:
            return StreamingResponse(
                _stream_hf_chat(payload),
                media_type="text/event-stream",
            )
        return await _chat_nonstream_hf(payload)


@router.get("/mcp-servers")
async def list_mcp_servers() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the active MCP server manifest for this Space."""
    return generate_mcp_server_manifest()


@router.get("/models")
async def list_models() -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    """List available inference backends."""
    models = [
        {"id": DEFAULT_MODEL, "provider": "huggingface", "name": "HF Default"},
        {"id": "llama3", "provider": "ollama", "name": "Ollama (local)"},
        {"id": "mistral", "provider": "ollama", "name": "Ollama Mistral"},
    ]
    return models


@router.post("/mcp-invoke")
async def invoke_mcp_tool(request: MCPInvokeRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Invoke an MCP tool by name with arguments."""
    manifest = generate_mcp_server_manifest()
    tool = next((t for t in manifest.get("tools", []) if t["name"] == request.tool_name), None)
    if not tool:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Tool {request.tool_name} not found")

    # Route to existing gbridge logic where possible
    if request.tool_name == "windsurf_generate_packet":
        from app.windsurf.packet import generate_windsurf_packet
        return {"result": generate_windsurf_packet()}

    if request.tool_name == "gbridge_list_leases":
        from app.gbridge.leases import list_active_leases
        leases = list_active_leases()
        return {"result": [{k: (v.isoformat() if hasattr(v, 'isoformat') else (v.value if hasattr(v, 'value') else v)) for k, v in l.__dict__.items()} for l in leases]}

    if request.tool_name == "gbridge_submit_job":
        from app.gbridge.router import route_capability_call
        cap = request.arguments.get("capability_name", "")
        payload = request.arguments.get("payload", {})
        session_id = request.arguments.get("session_id")
        return {"result": route_capability_call(cap, payload, session_id)}

    if request.tool_name == "gbridge_verify_receipt":
        from app.gbridge.receipts import load_receipt, verify_receipt_hash
        receipt_id = request.arguments.get("receipt_id", "")
        receipt = load_receipt(receipt_id)
        if not receipt:
            return {"result": None, "valid": False, "error": "receipt not found"}
        return {"result": {"valid": verify_receipt_hash(receipt), "receipt_id": receipt_id}}

    return {"result": None, "message": f"Tool {request.tool_name} is registered but no handler is implemented yet."}

routes_health.py:
```
import os
from fastapi import APIRouter
import httpx

router = APIRouter()

HF_API_URL = os.getenv("HF_INFERENCE_URL", "https://api-inference.huggingface.co/models/")
HF_API_TOKEN = os.getenv("HF_API_TOKEN", "")
DEFAULT_MODEL = os.getenv("HF_CHAT_MODEL", "meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct")
OLLAMA_URL = os.getenv("OLLAMA_URL", "http://localhost:11434")


@router.get("/health")
async def healthcheck():
    return {"status": "ok", "service": "gbridge-phonegpu-space"}


@router.get("/health/llm")
async def llm_healthcheck():
    """Check connectivity to available LLM backends."""
    results = {
        "huggingface": {"available": False, "error": None, "model": DEFAULT_MODEL},
        "ollama": {"available": False, "error": None, "models": []},
    }

    # Check HF Inference API
    if HF_API_TOKEN:
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10.0) as client:
                url = f"{HF_API_URL}{DEFAULT_MODEL}/v1/chat/completions"
                headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {HF_API_TOKEN}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}
                payload = {
                    "model": DEFAULT_MODEL,
                    "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}],
                    "max_tokens": 1,
                    "stream": False,
                }
                resp = await client.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
                if resp.status_code in (200, 422, 400):
                    results["huggingface"]["available"] = True
                else:
                    results["huggingface"]["error"] = f"Status {resp.status_code}"
        except Exception as e:
            results["huggingface"]["error"] = str(e)
    else:
        results["huggingface"]["error"] = "No HF_API_TOKEN configured"

    # Check Ollama
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=5.0) as client:
            resp = await client.get(f"{OLLAMA_URL}/api/tags")
            if resp.status_code == 200:
                data = resp.json()
                models = data.get("models", [])
                results["ollama"]["available"] = True
                results["ollama"]["models"] = [m.get("name", "") for m in models]
            else:
                results["ollama"]["error"] = f"Status {resp.status_code}"
    except Exception as e:
        results["ollama"]["error"] = str(e)

    any_available = results["huggingface"]["available"] or results["ollama"]["available"]
    return {"status": "ok" if any_available else "degraded", "backends": results}

leases.py:
```
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

from app.core.models import GBridgeLease, NodeType, TrustLevel, JobType
from app.core.security import generate_lease_id
from app.core.storage import save_lease

_leases_by_id: Dict[str, GBridgeLease] = {}


def create_lease(
    node_type: NodeType,
    node_id: str,
    capability_name: str,
    ttl_seconds: int = 3600,
    trust_level: TrustLevel = TrustLevel.TRUSTED_DEVICE,
    public_key: Optional[str] = None,
    allowed_job_types: Optional[List[JobType]] = None,
    receipt_required: bool = True,
) -> GBridgeLease:
    lease_id = generate_lease_id()
    now = datetime.utcnow()
    lease = GBridgeLease(
        lease_id=lease_id,
        node_type=node_type,
        node_id=node_id,
        capability_name=capability_name,
        endpoint_path=f"/cap/{capability_name}",
        trust_level=trust_level,
        created_at=now,
        expires_at=now + timedelta(seconds=ttl_seconds),
        public_key=public_key,
        allowed_job_types=allowed_job_types or [],
        receipt_required=receipt_required,
    )
    _leases_by_id[lease_id] = lease
    save_lease({k: v.isoformat() if isinstance(v, datetime) else v for k, v in lease.__dict__.items()})
    return lease


def get_lease(lease_id: str) -> Optional[GBridgeLease]:
    return _leases_by_id.get(lease_id)


def list_active_leases() -> List[GBridgeLease]:
    now = datetime.utcnow()
    return [l for l in _leases_by_id.values() if l.expires_at > now and l.status == "active"]


def list_leases_for_session(session_id: str) -> List[GBridgeLease]:
    # v1: return all active leases; filter by session in v2
    return list_active_leases()


def expire_leases() -> None:
    now = datetime.utcnow()
    for lease_id, lease in _leases_by_id.items():
        if lease.expires_at <= now and lease.status == "active":
            lease.status = "expired"


def revoke_lease(lease_id: str) -> bool:
    lease = _leases_by_id.get(lease_id)
    if lease:
        lease.status = "revoked"
        return True
    return False


def renew_lease(lease_id: str, ttl_seconds: int = 3600) -> bool:
    lease = _leases_by_id.get(lease_id)
    if lease and lease.status == "active":
        lease.expires_at = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(seconds=ttl_seconds)
        return True
    return False


def bind_lease_to_endpoint(lease_id: str) -> Optional[str]:
    lease = get_lease(lease_id)
    return lease.endpoint_path if lease else None

receipts.py:
```
import hashlib
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any

from app.core.models import ComputeReceipt, InferenceJob, WorkerState, JobResult, ReceiptVerificationResult
from app.core.security import generate_receipt_id, canonicalize_receipt_for_signing, verify_device_signature
from app.core.storage import save_receipt

_receipts_by_id: Dict[str, ComputeReceipt] = {}


def create_receipt(job: InferenceJob, worker: WorkerState, result: JobResult) -> ComputeReceipt:
    receipt_id = generate_receipt_id()
    receipt = ComputeReceipt(
        receipt_id=receipt_id,
        session_id=job.session_id,
        job_id=job.job_id,
        worker_id=worker.worker_id,
        capability=job.job_type.value,
        runtime_type=worker.runtime_type,
        job_type=job.job_type,
        privacy_mode=result.privacy_mode,
        latency_ms=result.latency_ms,
        started_at=job.assigned_at or job.created_at,
        finished_at=job.completed_at or datetime.utcnow(),
        model_id=next((c.model_id for c in worker.capabilities if c.capability_name == job.job_type.value), None),
        input_hash=result.input_hash,
        output_hash=result.output_hash,
        device_public_key=worker.device_public_key,
        device_signature=result.device_signature,
        previous_receipt_hash=_get_last_receipt_hash(job.session_id),
    )
    canonical = canonicalize_receipt_for_signing(receipt.__dict__)
    receipt.receipt_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
    server_msg = f"{receipt.receipt_hash}:{datetime.utcnow().isoformat()}"
    receipt.server_signature = hashlib.sha256(server_msg.encode()).hexdigest()
    _receipts_by_id[receipt_id] = receipt
    save_receipt({k: _serialize(v) for k, v in receipt.__dict__.items()})
    return receipt


def _get_last_receipt_hash(session_id: str) -> Optional[str]:
    recent = [r for r in _receipts_by_id.values() if r.session_id == session_id]
    if recent:
        recent.sort(key=lambda x: x.finished_at, reverse=True)
        return recent[0].receipt_hash
    return None


def _serialize(v: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(v, datetime):
        return v.isoformat()
    return v


def hash_receipt(receipt: ComputeReceipt) -> str:
    canonical = canonicalize_receipt_for_signing(receipt.__dict__)
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()


def verify_receipt_hash(receipt: ComputeReceipt) -> bool:
    return receipt.receipt_hash == hash_receipt(receipt)


def verify_device_receipt_signature(receipt: ComputeReceipt) -> bool:
    if not receipt.device_signature or not receipt.device_public_key:
        return True
    canonical = canonicalize_receipt_for_signing(receipt.__dict__)
    return verify_device_signature(receipt.device_public_key, canonical, receipt.device_signature)


def verify_server_receipt_signature(receipt: ComputeReceipt) -> bool:
    if not receipt.server_signature or not receipt.receipt_hash:
        return False
    server_msg = f"{receipt.receipt_hash}:{receipt.finished_at.isoformat() if receipt.finished_at else ''}"
    expected = hashlib.sha256(server_msg.encode()).hexdigest()
    return receipt.server_signature == expected


def load_receipt(receipt_id: str) -> Optional[ComputeReceipt]:
    return _receipts_by_id.get(receipt_id)


def list_receipts() -> list:
    return sorted(_receipts_by_id.values(), key=lambda r: r.finished_at or r.started_at, reverse=True)


def get_receipt_public_view(receipt_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    receipt = load_receipt(receipt_id)
    if not receipt:
        return None
    return {
        "receipt_id": receipt.receipt_id,
        "session_id": receipt.session_id,
        "job_id": receipt.job_id,
        "job_type": receipt.job_type.value,
        "capability": receipt.capability,
        "runtime_type": receipt.runtime_type.value,
        "model_id": receipt.model_id,
        "input_hash": receipt.input_hash,
        "output_hash": receipt.output_hash,
        "privacy_mode": receipt.privacy_mode.value,
        "latency_ms": receipt.latency_ms,
        "started_at": receipt.started_at.isoformat() if receipt.started_at else None,
        "finished_at": receipt.finished_at.isoformat() if receipt.finished_at else None,
        "device_public_key": receipt.device_public_key,
        "receipt_hash": receipt.receipt_hash,
        "previous_receipt_hash": receipt.previous_receipt_hash,
    }

```

protocol.py:

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from app.core.models import WorkerRuntimeType


def make_bridge_hello(node_id: str, node_type: str, capabilities: list) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"op": "bridge.hello", "node_id": node_id, "node_type": node_type, "capabilities": capabilities}


def make_capability_advertisement(node_id: str, capabilities: list) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"op": "capability.advertise", "node_id": node_id, "capabilities": capabilities}


def make_job_offer(job_id: str, job_type: str, payload: dict, privacy_mode: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"op": "job.offer", "job_id": job_id, "job_type": job_type, "payload": payload, "privacy_mode": privacy_mode}


def make_job_accept(job_id: str, worker_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"op": "job.accept", "job_id": job_id, "worker_id": worker_id}


def make_job_reject(job_id: str, worker_id: str, reason: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"op": "job.reject", "job_id": job_id, "worker_id": worker_id, "reason": reason}


def make_job_result(job_id: str, worker_id: str, output: dict, latency_ms: int, device_signature: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"op": "job.result", "job_id": job_id, "worker_id": worker_id, "output": output, "latency_ms": latency_ms, "device_signature": device_signature}


def make_receipt_message(receipt: dict) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"op": "receipt.emit", "receipt": receipt}


def make_agent_packet(node_id: str, packet_type: str, payload: dict) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"op": "agent.packet", "node_id": node_id, "packet_type": packet_type, "payload": payload}


def validate_bridge_message(msg: dict) -> bool:
    valid_ops = {
        "bridge.hello", "capability.advertise", "lease.create", "job.offer",
        "job.accept", "job.reject", "job.progress", "job.result",
        "receipt.emit", "agent.packet", "error", "disconnect",
    }
    return isinstance(msg, dict) and msg.get("op") in valid_ops


def parse_bridge_message(msg: dict) -> tuple[str, Optional[str], dict]:
    return msg.get("op", ""), msg.get("node_id"), msg

mcp_manifest.py:

from typing import Dict, Any, List

from app.gbridge.capabilities import list_all_capabilities, capability_to_openapi_operation, get_node_type_for_capability


def generate_mcp_server_manifest() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "mcp_version": "2024-11-05",
        "server_name": "gbridge-phonegpu",
        "server_version": "0.1.0",
        "description": "GBridge PhoneGPU — capability-to-endpoint lease layer for edge ML and agentic engineering",
        "tools": generate_mcp_tools_from_capabilities(),
    }


def generate_mcp_tools_from_capabilities() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    tools = []
    for cap in list_all_capabilities():
        tools.append(generate_tool_schema_for_capability(cap))
    # Add gbridge meta tools
    tools.append({
        "name": "gbridge_list_leases",
        "description": "List all active capability leases",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {}},
    })
    tools.append({
        "name": "gbridge_submit_job",
        "description": "Submit a job to a leased capability",
        "input_schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "capability_name": {"type": "string"},
                "payload": {"type": "object"},
            },
            "required": ["capability_name"],
        },
    })
    tools.append({
        "name": "gbridge_verify_receipt",
        "description": "Verify a compute receipt",
        "input_schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "receipt_id": {"type": "string"},
            },
            "required": ["receipt_id"],
        },
    })
    tools.append({
        "name": "windsurf_generate_packet",
        "description": "Generate a Windsurf build packet from current Space state",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {}},
    })
    return tools


def generate_tool_schema_for_capability(capability_name: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    node_type = get_node_type_for_capability(capability_name)
    return {
        "name": capability_name.replace(".", "_"),
        "description": f"Invoke {capability_name} via gbridge lease. Node: {node_type.value if node_type else 'unknown'}",
        "input_schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "payload": {"type": "object", "description": "Job payload"},
                "privacy_mode": {"type": "string", "enum": ["raw_input_remote", "hash_only_remote", "local_only_result_only"]},
            },
            "required": ["payload"],
        },
    }


def generate_windsurf_mcp_config() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "name": "gbridge-phonegpu",
        "version": "0.1.0",
        "description": "GBridge PhoneGPU MCP integration",
        "transport": {
            "type": "sse",
            "url": "https://josephrw-phonegpu-space.hf.space/api/windsurf/mcp",
        },
        "tools": generate_mcp_tools_from_capabilities(),
    }

capabilities.py:
```
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

from app.core.models import DeviceCapability, JobType, WorkerRuntimeType, NodeType

_capability_registry: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, any]]] = {}

CAPABILITY_NAMES = {
    JobType.TEXT_EMBEDDING: "iphone.text.embed.private",
    JobType.IMAGE_CLASSIFICATION: "iphone.image.classify.local",
    JobType.IMAGE_EMBEDDING: "iphone.image.embed.local",
    JobType.LOCAL_OCR: "iphone.ocr.local",
    JobType.AUDIO_TRANSCRIPTION: "iphone.audio.transcribe.local",
    JobType.SMALL_LLM_GENERATE: "iphone.llm.generate.local",
    JobType.PRIVACY_REDACTION: "iphone.privacy.redact.local",
    JobType.SENSOR_CLASSIFICATION: "iphone.sensor.motion.classify",
}

NODE_CAPABILITIES = {
    NodeType.IPHONE_WORKER: [
        "iphone.text.embed.private",
        "iphone.image.classify.local",
        "iphone.privacy.redact.local",
        "iphone.text.echo.private",
    ],
    NodeType.WINDSURF_AGENT: [
        "windsurf.repo.inspect",
        "windsurf.patch.generate",
        "windsurf.packet.export",
        "windsurf.build.verify",
    ],
    NodeType.HF_SPACE: [
        "space.receipt.verify",
        "space.job.route",
        "space.session.create",
    ],
    NodeType.MCP_TOOL: [
        "mcp.tool.invoke",
        "mcp.tool.list",
    ],
}


def normalize_capability_name(raw: str) -> str:
    return raw.strip().lower().replace(" ", ".")


def validate_capability_name(name: str) -> bool:
    parts = name.split(".")
    return len(parts) >= 3 and parts[0] in ["iphone", "windsurf", "space", "mcp", "browser"]


def register_capabilities(session_id: str, node_id: str, capabilities: List[dict]) -> bool:
    key = f"{session_id}:{node_id}"
    _capability_registry[key] = capabilities
    return True


def get_capabilities(session_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, any]]:
    result = []
    for key, caps in _capability_registry.items():
        if key.startswith(f"{session_id}:"):
            for c in caps:
                result.append({**c, "source_worker": key.split(":", 1)[1]})
    return result


def find_capability(name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, any]]:
    name = normalize_capability_name(name)
    for key, caps in _capability_registry.items():
        for c in caps:
            if c.get("capability_name") == name:
                return {**c, "source_worker": key.split(":", 1)[1]}
    return None


def list_all_capabilities() -> List[str]:
    return list(CAPABILITY_NAMES.values()) + [
        "windsurf.repo.inspect",
        "windsurf.patch.generate",
        "windsurf.packet.export",
        "windsurf.build.verify",
        "space.receipt.verify",
        "space.job.route",
        "space.session.create",
        "iphone.text.echo.private",
    ]


def capability_to_route_path(name: str) -> str:
    return f"/cap/{name}"


def capability_to_openapi_operation(name: str) -> Dict[str, any]:
    return {
        "operationId": name.replace(".", "_"),
        "summary": f"Invoke {name}",
        "tags": [name.split(".")[0]],
        "requestBody": {"required": True, "content": {"application/json": {"schema": {"type": "object"}}}},
        "responses": {"200": {"description": "Result with receipt"}},
    }


def get_node_type_for_capability(name: str) -> Optional[NodeType]:
    prefix = name.split(".")[0]
    mapping = {
        "iphone": NodeType.IPHONE_WORKER,
        "windsurf": NodeType.WINDSURF_AGENT,
        "space": NodeType.HF_SPACE,
        "mcp": NodeType.MCP_TOOL,
        "browser": NodeType.BROWSER_WORKER,
    }
    return mapping.get(prefix)

router.py:

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any

from app.core.models import InferenceJob, JobType, PrivacyMode
from app.gbridge.leases import list_active_leases, get_lease
from app.gbridge.capabilities import find_capability, get_node_type_for_capability
from app.phone.sessions import get_session
from app.phone.workers import list_workers_for_session
from app.phone.jobs import create_job, assign_job_to_worker


def route_capability_call(capability_name: str, payload: dict, session_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Core gbridge primitive:
    1. Find active lease for capability
    2. Verify lease is not expired
    3. Check payload policy
    4. Create job
    5. Route to correct node
    6. Return async job id
    """
    # Find lease for capability
    leases = [l for l in list_active_leases() if l.capability_name == capability_name]
    if not leases:
        return {"error": "capability_unavailable", "capability": capability_name, "job_id": None}
    
    lease = leases[0]  # v1: pick first available
    
    # Determine job type from capability
    job_type = _capability_to_job_type(capability_name)
    if not job_type:
        return {"error": "unknown_capability", "capability": capability_name}
    
    # Create job
    if session_id:
        job = create_job(session_id, job_type, payload, PrivacyMode.RAW_INPUT_REMOTE, {})
        # Try to assign to worker matching lease
        workers = list_workers_for_session(session_id)
        matching = [w for w in workers if any(c.capability_name == capability_name for c in w.capabilities)]
        if matching:
            assign_job_to_worker(job.job_id, matching[0].worker_id)
        return {
            "job_id": job.job_id,
            "status": job.status.value,
            "capability": capability_name,
            "endpoint": lease.endpoint_path,
            "lease_id": lease.lease_id,
        }
    
    return {"error": "no_session", "capability": capability_name}


def select_node_for_capability(capability_name: str, session_id: str) -> Optional[str]:
    workers = list_workers_for_session(session_id)
    for w in workers:
        for c in w.capabilities:
            if c.capability_name == capability_name:
                return w.worker_id
    return None


def reject_unroutable_call(job: InferenceJob, reason: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    from app.phone.jobs import reject_job
    reject_job(job.job_id, reason)
    return {"error": reason, "job_id": job.job_id}


def _capability_to_job_type(capability_name: str) -> Optional[JobType]:
    mapping = {
        "iphone.text.embed.private": JobType.TEXT_EMBEDDING,
        "iphone.text.echo.private": JobType.TEXT_EMBEDDING,
        "iphone.image.classify.local": JobType.IMAGE_CLASSIFICATION,
        "iphone.privacy.redact.local": JobType.PRIVACY_REDACTION,
    }
    return mapping.get(capability_name)

```

from app.gbridge.capabilities import list_all_capabilities
from app.gbridge.leases import list_active_leases
from app.phone.sessions import list_all_sessions
from app.phone.workers import list_workers_for_session


def render_phone_page(request: Request) -> HTMLResponse:
    caps = list_all_capabilities()
    leases = list_active_leases()
    sessions = list_all_sessions()
    total_workers = sum(len(list_workers_for_session(s.session_id)) for s in sessions)
    return HTMLResponse(content=_phone_html(caps, leases, sessions, total_workers), status_code=200)

receipts.py:

from fastapi import Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse
from app.gbridge.receipts import list_receipts


def render_receipts_page(request: Request) -> HTMLResponse:
    receipts = list_receipts()
    total = len(receipts)
    avg_latency = int(sum(r.latency_ms for r in receipts) / total) if total else 0
    return HTMLResponse(content=_receipts_html(total, avg_latency), status_code=200)


def _receipts_html(total: int, avg_latency: int) -> str:

async function loadReceipts(){try{const r=await fetch('/api/receipts');const d=await r.json();const list=document.getElementById('receiptsList');if(!d||d.length===0){list.innerHTML='<div style="color:var(--muted);padding:20px;text-align:center">No receipts yet. Run a job to generate one.</div>';return;}list.innerHTML=d.map(rc=>'<div class="receipt"><div class="receipt-header"><span class="receipt-id">'+(rc.receipt_id?rc.receipt_id.slice(0,16):'N/A')+'...</span><span class="badge badge-verified">Verified</span></div><div class="receipt-row"><span class="receipt-label">Job</span><span class="receipt-value">'+rc.job_type+'</span></div><div class="receipt-row"><span class="receipt-label">Capability</span><span class="receipt-value">'+rc.capability+'</span></div><div class="receipt-row"><span class="receipt-label">Latency</span><span class="receipt-value">'+rc.latency_ms+'ms</span></div><div class="receipt-row"><span class="receipt-label">Hash</span><span class="receipt-value">'+(rc.receipt_hash?rc.receipt_hash.slice(0,16):'N/A')+'...</span></div></div>').join('');}catch(e){document.getElementById('receiptsList').innerHTML='<div style="color:var(--muted)">Error loading receipts</div>';}}
async function verifyReceipt(){const id=document.getElementById('verifyInput').value;if(!id)return;try{const r=await fetch('/api/receipts/'+id+'/verify',{method:'POST'});const d=await r.json();const bg=d.valid?'rgba(52,211,153,.08)':'rgba(248,113,113,.08)';const border=d.valid?'rgba(52,211,153,.2)':'rgba(248,113,113,.2)';const color=d.valid?'var(--green)':'#f87171';const label=d.valid?'Valid':'Invalid';const dev=d.device_signature_valid===null?'N/A':(d.device_signature_valid?'valid':'invalid');const srv=d.server_signature_valid?'valid':'invalid';document.getElementById('verifyResult').innerHTML='<div style="padding:12px;border-radius:10px;background:'+bg+';border:1px solid '+border+';margin-top:12px"><div style="font-weight:600;color:'+color+'">'+(d.valid?'&#10003; ':'&#10007; ')+label+'</div><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:4px">Hash: '+(d.hash_match?'match':'mismatch')+' | Device: '+dev+' | Server: '+srv+'</div></div>';}catch(e){document.getElementById('verifyResult').innerHTML='<div style="color:#f87171">Verification failed</div>';}}
loadReceipts();setInterval(loadReceipts,5000);
</script>

</html>"""
    return html.replace("_TOTAL_", str(total)).replace("_LATENCY_", str(avg_latency))

landing.py:

from fastapi import Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse
from app.gbridge.capabilities import list_all_capabilities
from app.gbridge.leases import list_active_leases
from app.gbridge.receipts import list_receipts
from app.phone.sessions import list_all_sessions


def render_landing() -> HTMLResponse:
    cap_count = len(list_all_capabilities())
    lease_count = len(list_active_leases())
    receipt_count = len(list_receipts())
    session_count = len(list_all_sessions())
    return HTMLResponse(content=_landing_html(cap_count, lease_count, receipt_count, session_count), status_code=200)

<script>
let sessionId = '';
let token = '';
let messageHistory = [];

function escapeHtml(t) {{ const d = document.createElement('div'); d.textContent = t; return d.innerHTML; }}

async function createSession() {{
  const btn = document.getElementById('createSessionBtn');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Creating...';
  try {{
    const r = await fetch('/api/gbridge/session', {{method:'POST',headers:{{'Content-Type':'application/json'}},body:JSON.stringify({{}})}});
    const d = await r.json();
    sessionId = d.session_id; token = d.secret || d.join_token;
    document.getElementById('qrDisplay').innerHTML = '<img src="data:image/png;base64,' + d.qr_code_base64 + '"><div class="qr-label">Scan with iPhone camera</div>';
    const info = document.getElementById('sessionInfo');
    info.style.display = 'block';
    info.innerHTML = '<strong>Session:</strong> ' + sessionId.slice(0,16) + '...<br><strong>Token:</strong> ' + token.slice(0,16) + '...';
    refreshStatus();
  }} catch(e) {{
    document.getElementById('qrDisplay').innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--red)">Failed: ' + e.message + '</div>';
  }} finally {{ btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Create Session'; }}
}}

async function sendChat() {{
  const input = document.getElementById('chatInput');
  const btn = document.getElementById('chatSend');
  const text = input.value.trim();
  if(!text) return;
  input.value = ''; btn.disabled = true;
  const msgs = document.getElementById('chatMessages');
  msgs.innerHTML += '<div class="msg user">' + escapeHtml(text) + '</div>';
  msgs.innerHTML += '<div class="msg assistant typing" id="typing"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div>';
  msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight;
  messageHistory.push({{role:'user',content:text}});

  try {{
    const r = await fetch('/api/chat/completions', {{method:'POST',headers:{{'Content-Type':'application/json'}},body:JSON.stringify({{messages:messageHistory,stream:true,max_tokens:512}})}});
    const reader = r.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    let assistantText = '';
    let started = false;
    document.getElementById('typing').remove();

    while(true) {{
      const {{value,done}} = await reader.read();
      if(done) break;
      const chunk = decoder.decode(value,{{stream:true}});
      const lines = chunk.split('\n');
      for(const line of lines) {{
        if(!line.startsWith('data: ')) continue;
        const data = line.slice(6).trim();
        if(!data) continue;
        if(data === '[DONE]') break;
        try {{
          const obj = JSON.parse(data);
          if(obj.error) {{
            if(!started) {{ msgs.innerHTML += '<div class="msg error">Error: ' + escapeHtml(obj.error) + '</div>'; started = true; }}
            else {{ appendToLast('\nError: ' + obj.error); }}
            break;
          }}
          if(obj.done) break;
          if(obj.content) {{
            if(!started) {{ msgs.innerHTML += '<div class="msg assistant" id="currentMsg">' + escapeHtml(obj.content) + '</div>'; started = true; }}
            else {{ appendToLast(obj.content); }}
            assistantText += obj.content;
          }}
        }} catch(e) {{}}
      }}
    }}
    messageHistory.push({{role:'assistant',content:assistantText || '(no response)'}});
  }} catch(err) {{
    document.getElementById('typing')?.remove();
    msgs.innerHTML += '<div class="msg error">Error: ' + escapeHtml(err.message) + '</div>';
  }} finally {{
    btn.disabled = false;
    msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight;
  }}
}}

function appendToLast(text) {{
  const el = document.getElementById('currentMsg');
  if(el) el.innerHTML += escapeHtml(text);
}}

async function refreshStatus() {{
  try {{
    const r = await fetch('/health/llm');
    const d = await r.json();
    const container = document.getElementById('llmStatus');
    const hf = d.backends.huggingface;
    const oll = d.backends.ollama;
    document.getElementById('llmDot').style.background = (hf.available || oll.available) ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
    document.getElementById('llmDot').style.boxShadow = (hf.available || oll.available) ? '0 0 6px var(--green)' : '0 0 6px var(--red)';
    let html = '';
    html += '<div class="backend-row"><span class="name">Hugging Face</span><span class="badge ' + (hf.available ? 'badge-ok' : 'badge-off') + '">' + (hf.available ? 'Online' : 'Offline') + '</span></div>';
    if(hf.error && !hf.available) html += '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--red);padding:0 12px 8px">' + escapeHtml(hf.error) + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="backend-row"><span class="name">Ollama (Local)</span><span class="badge ' + (oll.available ? 'badge-ok' : 'badge-off') + '">' + (oll.available ? 'Online' : 'Offline') + '</span></div>';
    if(oll.available && oll.models.length) html += '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);padding:0 12px 8px">Models: ' + oll.models.slice(0,3).join(', ') + '</div>';
    if(oll.error && !oll.available) html += '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--red);padding:0 12px 8px">' + escapeHtml(oll.error) + '</div>';
    container.innerHTML = html;
  }} catch(e) {{ console.error('LLM status error', e); }}

  if(sessionId) {{
    try {{
      const r = await fetch('/api/gbridge/session/' + sessionId);
      if(r.ok) {{
        const d = await r.json();
        const wl = document.getElementById('workerList');
        if(d.worker_count > 0) {{
          wl.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--green);font-size:12px;padding:8px 0">' + d.worker_count + ' worker(s) connected</div>';
          document.getElementById('workerDot').style.background = 'var(--green)';
          document.getElementById('workerDot').style.boxShadow = '0 0 6px var(--green)';
        }} else {{
          wl.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;text-align:center;padding:20px">No workers yet. Scan QR with your device.</div>';
          document.getElementById('workerDot').style.background = 'var(--amber)';
          document.getElementById('workerDot').style.boxShadow = '0 0 6px var(--amber)';
        }}
      }}
    }} catch(e) {{}}
  }}

  // Fetch mesh topology
  try {{
    const r = await fetch('/api/mesh/nodes');
    const nodes = await r.json();
    const mt = document.getElementById('meshTopology');
    const ms = await fetch('/api/mesh/status');
    const meshStats = await ms.json();
    document.getElementById('meshTps').textContent = meshStats.combined_tokens_per_second || 0;
    if(nodes.length === 0) {{
      mt.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;text-align:center;padding:20px">No nodes in mesh. Connect a device to build the cluster.</div>';
      document.getElementById('meshDot').style.background = 'var(--muted)';
    }} else {{
      let html = '<div class="mesh-grid">';
      for(const n of nodes) {{
        const icon = n.node_type === 'mac_agent' ? '&#128187;' : '&#128241;';
        const statusColor = n.status === 'online' ? 'var(--green)' : (n.status === 'busy' ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--red)');
        html += '<div class="mesh-node">';
        html += '<div class="node-icon">' + icon + '</div>';
        html += '<div class="node-name">' + escapeHtml(n.node_id.slice(0,12)) + '...</div>';
        html += '<div class="node-type">' + n.node_type + '</div>';
        html += '<div><span class="node-status" style="background:' + statusColor + '"></span>' + n.status + '</div>';
        if(n.tokens_per_second > 0) html += '<div class="node-tps">' + n.tokens_per_second.toFixed(1) + ' t/s</div>';
        html += '</div>';
      }}
      html += '</div>';
      mt.innerHTML = html;
      const anyOnline = nodes.some(n => n.status === 'online' || n.status === 'busy');
      document.getElementById('meshDot').style.background = anyOnline ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
      document.getElementById('meshDot').style.boxShadow = anyOnline ? '0 0 6px var(--green)' : '0 0 6px var(--red)';
    }}
  }} catch(e) {{ console.error('Mesh status error', e); }}
}}

refreshStatus();
setInterval(refreshStatus, 10000);
</script>
</body>
</html>"""

def render_docs():
    return """

            <div class="logo"><span>M5</span> Documentation</div>

                <a href="/m5-landing">Home</a>
                <a href="/api">API Reference</a>
                <a href="/sdk">SDK</a>
                <a href="/m5">Dashboard</a>
            </nav>
        </header>
        
        <div class="docs-layout">
            <aside class="sidebar">
                <h3>Getting Started</h3>
                <a href="#introduction" class="active">Introduction</a>
                <a href="#authentication">Authentication</a>
                <a href="#quickstart">Quick Start</a>
                
                <h3 style="margin-top: 24px;">API Reference</h3>
                <a href="#chat-api">Chat Completions</a>
                <a href="#models-api">Models</a>
                <a href="#accounts-api">Accounts</a>
                
                <h3 style="margin-top: 24px;">SDKs</h3>
                <a href="#python-sdk">Python SDK</a>
                <a href="#js-sdk">JavaScript SDK</a>
                
                <h3 style="margin-top: 24px;">Advanced</h3>
                <a href="#tunneling">Tunneling</a>
                <a href="#rate-limits">Rate Limits</a>
            </aside>

                <h1 id="introduction">Introduction</h1>
                <p>M5 is a local compute gateway that turns your Ollama instance into a public API. It provides OpenAI-compatible endpoints, chat storage, and API key management.</p>
                
                <h2 id="authentication">Authentication</h2>
                <p>M5 uses Google OAuth for authentication. Sign in with your Google account to get started. After signing in, you'll receive an API key for programmatic access.</p>
                
                <h2 id="quickstart">Quick Start</h2>
                <p>1. Install Ollama locally:</p>
                <pre><code>curl https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh</code></pre>
                
                <p>2. Pull a model:</p>
                <pre><code>ollama pull llama3.2</code></pre>
                
                <p>3. Sign in to M5 and get your API key</p>
                
                <p>4. Make a request:</p>
                <pre><code>curl -X POST https://m5.hf.space/v1/chat/completions \\
  -H "Authorization: Bearer YOUR_API_KEY" \\
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \\
  -d '{"model":"llama3.2","messages":[{"role":"user","content":"Hello"}]}'</code></pre>
                
                <h1 id="chat-api">Chat Completions API</h1>
                <p>OpenAI-compatible chat completions endpoint.</p>
                
                <h3>Endpoint</h3>
                <code>POST /v1/chat/completions</code>
                
                <h3>Request Body</h3>
                <pre><code>{
  "model": "llama3.2",
  "messages": [
    {"role": "user", "content": "Hello"}
  ],
  "temperature": 0.7,
  "max_tokens": 1000,
  "stream": false,
  "ollama_url": "http://localhost:11434"  // Optional
}</code></pre>
                
                <h3>Response</h3>
                <pre><code>{
  "id": "chatcmpl-xxx",
  "object": "chat.completion",
  "created": 1234567890,
  "model": "llama3.2",
  "choices": [
    {
      "index": 0,
      "message": {
        "role": "assistant",
        "content": "Hello! How can I help you?"
      },
      "finish_reason": "stop"
    }
  ],
  "usage": {
    "prompt_tokens": 10,
    "completion_tokens": 20,
    "total_tokens": 30
  }
}</code></pre>
                
                <h1 id="models-api">Models API</h1>
                <p>List available Ollama models.</p>
                
                <h3>Endpoint</h3>
                <code>GET /v1/models</code>
                
                <h3>Response</h3>
                <pre><code>{
  "object": "list",
  "data": [
    {
      "id": "llama3.2",
      "object": "model",
      "created": 1234567890,
      "owned_by": "ollama"
    }
  ]
}</code></pre>
                
                <h1 id="accounts-api">Accounts API</h1>
                <p>Manage your account and API keys.</p>
                
                <h3>Get Account Info</h3>
                <code>GET /api/accounts/me</code>
                
                <h3>Create API Key</h3>
                <code>POST /api/accounts/api-keys</code>
                
                <h1 id="python-sdk">Python SDK</h1>
                <pre><code>from m5 import M5Client

client = M5Client(api_key="your-api-key")

response = client.chat.completions.create(
    model="llama3.2",
    messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "Hello"}]
)

print(response.choices[0].message.content)</code></pre>
                
                <h1 id="tunneling">Tunneling</h1>
                <p>Expose your local Ollama to the cloud using ngrok:</p>
                <pre><code># Start ngrok
ngrok http 11434

# Register tunnel in M5 UI or API
POST /api/accounts/ollama-tunnel
{
  "ollama_public_url": "https://xxx.ngrok-free.app"
}</code></pre>
                
                <h1 id="rate-limits">Rate Limits</h1>
                <ul>
                    <li>60 requests per minute</li>
                    <li>1000 requests per day</li>
                    <li>Custom limits available for enterprise</li>
                </ul>
            </main>
        </div>
    </div>
</body>
</html>
"""

"""
Public Chat Viewer - Share chats via public URLs
"""
from fastapi import Request


def render_public_chat():
    return """
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Shared Chat - M5</title>
    <style>
        :root {
            --bg: #0a0a0f;
            --surface: #12121a;
            --surface-2: #1a1a24;
            --border: rgba(255,255,255,.08);
            --text: #f0f0f5;
            --muted: #6b6b78;
            --primary: #00f0ff;
        }
        * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
        body {
            font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'SF Pro Display', sans-serif;
            background: var(--bg);
            color: var(--text);
            min-height: 100vh;
        }
        .container { max-width: 800px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 40px 20px; }
        
        .header {
            text-align: center;
            margin-bottom: 40px;
        }
        .header h1 { font-size: 32px; margin-bottom: 8px; }
        .header p { color: var(--muted); }
        
        .chat-container {
            background: var(--surface);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: 16px;
            padding: 24px;
        }
        
        .message {
            margin-bottom: 20px;
            padding: 16px;
            border-radius: 12px;
        }
        .message.user {
            background: var(--surface-2);
            margin-left: 40px;
        }
        .message.assistant {
            background: rgba(0,240,255,.05);
            border: 1px solid rgba(0,240,255,.1);
            margin-right: 40px;
        }
        .message .role {
            font-size: 12px;
            color: var(--muted);
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
        }
        .message .content {
            line-height: 1.6;
            white-space: pre-wrap;
        }
        
        .loading {
            text-align: center;
            padding: 40px;
            color: var(--muted);
        }
        
        .error {
            text-align: center;
            padding: 40px;
            color: #f87171;
        }
        
        .footer {
            text-align: center;
            margin-top: 40px;
            color: var(--muted);
            font-size: 14px;
        }
        .footer a { color: var(--primary); text-decoration: none; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="header">
            <h1>Shared Chat</h1>
            <p>Powered by M5</p>
        </div>
        
        <div class="chat-container" id="chatContainer">
            <div class="loading">Loading chat...</div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="footer">
            <a href="/m5-landing">Create your own chat with M5</a>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <script>
        const publicId = window.location.pathname.split('/').pop();
        
        async function loadChat() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`/api/chats/public/${publicId}`);
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error('Chat not found or expired');
                }
                const data = await response.json();
                
                const container = document.getElementById('chatContainer');
                let html = `<h2 style="margin-bottom: 24px;">${data.title}</h2>`;
                
                data.messages.forEach(msg => {
                    html += `
                        <div class="message ${msg.role}">
                            <div class="role">${msg.role}</div>
                            <div class="content">${escapeHtml(msg.content)}</div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                });
                
                container.innerHTML = html;
            } catch (error) {
                document.getElementById('chatContainer').innerHTML = `
                    <div class="error">
                        ${error.message}
                    </div>
                `;
            }
        }
        
        function escapeHtml(text) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.textContent = text;
            return div.innerHTML;
        }
        
        loadChat();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
"""

```

gbridge.py:
```
from fastapi import Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse
from app.gbridge.capabilities import list_all_capabilities
from app.gbridge.leases import list_active_leases
from app.phone.sessions import list_all_sessions
from app.phone.workers import list_workers_for_session


def render_gbridge_page(request: Request) -> HTMLResponse:
    caps = list_all_capabilities()
    leases = list_active_leases()
    sessions = list_all_sessions()
    total_workers = sum(len(list_workers_for_session(s.session_id)) for s in sessions)
    return HTMLResponse(content=_gbridge_html(caps, leases, total_workers), status_code=200)


def _gbridge_html(caps, leases, total_workers) -> str:
    lease_rows = ''.join([
        f'<tr><td>{l.capability_name}</td><td><span class="badge badge-{l.node_type.value.split("_")[0]}">{l.node_type.value}</span></td><td class="endpoint">{l.endpoint_path}</td><td>{l.status}</td><td>{l.expires_at.strftime("%H:%M") if hasattr(l.expires_at, "strftime") else "N/A"}</td></tr>'
        for l in leases[:10]
    ]) if leases else '<tr><td colspan="5" style="color:var(--muted)">No active leases</td></tr>'
    cap_rows = ''.join([
        f'<tr><td class="endpoint">{c}</td><td><span class="badge badge-iphone">capability</span></td><td class="endpoint">/cap/{c}</td><td>POST</td></tr>'
        for c in caps[:10]
    ])
    return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">

<style>

*{{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0}}body{{font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,sans-serif;background:var(--bg);color:var(--text);min-height:100vh}}
.container{{max-width:1100px;margin:0 auto;padding:32px 24px}}

.subtitle{{color:var(--muted);font-size:14px;margin-bottom:32px;max-width:600px;line-height:1.5}}
.nav{{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;right:0;z-index:100;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:16px 24px;backdrop-filter:blur(20px);background:rgba(7,7,10,.8);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}}
.nav .logo{{font-weight:700;font-size:14px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px}}
.nav a{{color:var(--muted);text-decoration:none;font-size:13px;margin-left:20px;transition:.2s}}
.nav a:hover{{color:var(--text)}}
.card{{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;padding:24px;margin-bottom:16px}}
.card h3{{font-size:13px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:1.5px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:16px}}
.diagram{{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:32px;padding:32px;flex-wrap:wrap}}

.node.active{{border-color:var(--cyan);box-shadow:0 0 40px -15px rgba(0,240,255,.1)}}
.node .label{{color:var(--muted);font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:1px}}

@keyframes pulse{{0%,100%{{opacity:1;transform:scale(1)}}50%{{opacity:.4;transform:scale(1.5)}}}}

from fastapi import Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse


def render_chat_page(request: Request) -> HTMLResponse:
    return HTMLResponse(content=_chat_html(), status_code=200)

<script>
const messagesEl = document.getElementById('messages');
const inputEl = document.getElementById('input');
const sendBtn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
const modelSelect = document.getElementById('modelSelect');
const backendSelect = document.getElementById('backendSelect');
const mcpToolsEl = document.getElementById('mcpTools');
const statusDot = document.getElementById('statusDot');
const statusText = document.getElementById('statusText');
const modelSection = document.getElementById('modelSection');
const gbridgeSection = document.getElementById('gbridgeSection');
const createSessionBtn = document.getElementById('createSessionBtn');
const sessionInfo = document.getElementById('sessionInfo');
const qrContainer = document.getElementById('qrContainer');
const workersList = document.getElementById('workersList');

let currentModel = null;
let isOllama = false;
let useGBridge = false;
let sessionId = null;
let ws = null;
let messageHistory = [];
let workers = [];
let workerPollInterval = null;

function handleKey(e) {
  if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
    e.preventDefault();
    sendMessage();
  }
}

function addMessage(role, text, toolCall) {
  const empty = messagesEl.querySelector('.empty-state');
  if (empty) empty.remove();

  const div = document.createElement('div');
  div.className = `message ${role}`;
  const avatarText = role === 'user' ? 'You' : 'AI';
  const avatarClass = role === 'user' ? 'user' : 'assistant';
  let html = `<div class="avatar ${avatarClass}">${avatarText}</div><div class="bubble">${escapeHtml(text)}</div>`;
  if (toolCall) {
    html += `<div class="tool-call"><div class="tool-name">${escapeHtml(toolCall.name)}</div><div class="tool-args">${escapeHtml(JSON.stringify(toolCall.args))}</div></div>`;
  }
  div.innerHTML = html;
  messagesEl.appendChild(div);
  messagesEl.scrollTop = messagesEl.scrollHeight;
}

function appendToLast(text) {
  const msgs = messagesEl.querySelectorAll('.message.assistant .bubble');
  if (msgs.length === 0) return;
  const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1];
  last.textContent += text;
  messagesEl.scrollTop = messagesEl.scrollHeight;
}

function escapeHtml(t) {
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.textContent = t;
  return d.innerHTML;
}

async function sendMessage() {
  const text = inputEl.value.trim();
  if (!text) return;
  if (useGBridge && !sessionId) { alert('Create a GBridge session first'); return; }
  if (!useGBridge && !currentModel) { alert('Select a model first'); return; }

  inputEl.value = '';
  inputEl.rows = 1;
  addMessage('user', text);
  messageHistory.push({role:'user', content:text});
  sendBtn.disabled = true;
  statusText.textContent = 'Generating...';
  statusDot.className = 'status-dot status-online';

  try {
    if (useGBridge) {
      // Send via GBridge WebSocket
      if (ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        ws.send(JSON.stringify({
          op: 'chat_submit',
          prompt: text,
          max_tokens: 512,
          temperature: 0.7
        }));
      } else {
        throw new Error('WebSocket not connected');
      }
    } else {
      // Send via HF/Ollama API
      const res = await fetch('/api/chat/completions', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
        body: JSON.stringify({
          messages: messageHistory,
          model: currentModel,
          stream: true,
          use_ollama: isOllama,
        })
      });

      const reader = res.body.getReader();
      const decoder = new TextDecoder();
      let assistantText = '';
      let started = false;

      while (true) {
        const {value, done} = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        const chunk = decoder.decode(value, {stream:true});
        const lines = chunk.split('\\n');
        for (const line of lines) {
          if (!line.startsWith('data: ')) continue;
          const data = line.slice(6).trim();
          if (!data) continue;
          try {
            const obj = JSON.parse(data);
            if (obj.error) {
              if (!started) addMessage('assistant', 'Error: ' + obj.error);
              else appendToLast('\\nError: ' + obj.error);
              break;
            }
            if (obj.done) break;
            if (obj.content) {
              if (!started) { addMessage('assistant', obj.content); started = true; }
              else appendToLast(obj.content);
              assistantText += obj.content;
            }
          } catch (e) {}
        }
      }

      messageHistory.push({role:'assistant', content: assistantText || '(no response)'});
      statusText.textContent = 'Ready';
    }
  } catch (err) {
    addMessage('assistant', 'Error: ' + err.message);
    statusText.textContent = 'Error';
    statusDot.className = 'status-dot status-offline';
  } finally {
    sendBtn.disabled = false;
  }
}

async function loadModels() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/chat/models');
    const models = await res.json();
    modelSelect.innerHTML = '';
    for (const m of models) {
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = m.id;
      opt.textContent = `${m.name} (${m.provider})`;
      opt.dataset.provider = m.provider;
      modelSelect.appendChild(opt);
    }
    if (models.length) {
      currentModel = models[0].id;
      isOllama = models[0].provider === 'ollama';
      modelSelect.value = currentModel;
    }
    modelSelect.onchange = () => {
      currentModel = modelSelect.value;
      isOllama = modelSelect.options[modelSelect.selectedIndex].dataset.provider === 'ollama';
    };
  } catch (e) {
    modelSelect.innerHTML = '<option>Error loading models</option>';
  }
}

backendSelect.onchange = () => {
  const backend = backendSelect.value;
  useGBridge = backend === 'gbridge';
  isOllama = backend === 'ollama';
  
  if (useGBridge) {
    modelSection.style.display = 'none';
    gbridgeSection.style.display = 'block';
  } else {
    modelSection.style.display = 'block';
    gbridgeSection.style.display = 'none';
  }
};

createSessionBtn.onclick = async () => {
  try {
    createSessionBtn.disabled = true;
    createSessionBtn.textContent = 'Creating...';
    const res = await fetch('/api/gbridge/session', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({})
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    sessionId = data.session_id;
    sessionInfo.textContent = `Session: ${data.session_id.slice(0,16)}...`;
    
    if (data.qr_code_base64) {
      qrContainer.innerHTML = `<img src="data:image/png;base64,${data.qr_code_base64}" style="width:160px;height:160px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--border);">`;
    }
    
    createSessionBtn.textContent = 'Session Active';
    connectWebSocket();
    workerPollInterval = setInterval(fetchWorkers, 5000);
  } catch (err) {
    sessionInfo.textContent = 'Error: ' + err.message;
    createSessionBtn.disabled = false;
    createSessionBtn.textContent = 'Create Session';
  }
};

function connectWebSocket() {
  if (!sessionId) return;
  const protocol = location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
  const wsUrl = `${protocol}//${location.host}/ws/gbridge/client/${sessionId}`;
  ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl);
  
  ws.onopen = () => {
    statusText.textContent = 'Connected to GBridge';
    ws.send(JSON.stringify({op: 'client_hello', session_id: sessionId}));
  };
  
  ws.onmessage = (e) => {
    const msg = JSON.parse(e.data);
    if (msg.op === 'job_stream') {
      if (!messageHistory.find(m => m.role === 'assistant' && m.content.includes(msg.chunk))) {
        addMessage('assistant', msg.chunk);
      } else {
        appendToLast(msg.chunk);
      }
    } else if (msg.op === 'job_complete') {
      messageHistory.push({role:'assistant', content: msg.output?.text || '(completed)'});
      statusText.textContent = 'Ready';
      sendBtn.disabled = false;
    } else if (msg.op === 'error') {
      addMessage('assistant', 'Error: ' + msg.error);
      statusText.textContent = 'Error';
      sendBtn.disabled = false;
    } else if (msg.op === 'session_update') {
      sessionInfo.textContent = `Session: ${sessionId.slice(0,16)}... (${msg.worker_count} workers)`;
      fetchWorkers();
    }
  };
  
  ws.onclose = () => {
    statusText.textContent = 'Disconnected';
    statusDot.className = 'status-dot status-offline';
    if (workerPollInterval) {
      clearInterval(workerPollInterval);
      workerPollInterval = null;
    }
  };
  
  ws.onerror = () => {
    statusText.textContent = 'Connection error';
    statusDot.className = 'status-dot status-offline';
  };
}

async function fetchWorkers() {
  if (!sessionId) return;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/gbridge/session/${sessionId}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    workers = data.workers || [];
    renderWorkers();
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to fetch workers:', err);
  }
}

function renderWorkers() {
  if (workers.length === 0) {
    workersList.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);padding:8px;">No workers connected. Scan QR with iPhone.</div>';
    return;
  }
  
  let html = '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:8px;">Connected Workers:</div>';
  workers.forEach(w => {
    const isMac = w.runtime_type === 'ollama' || w.runtime_type === 'native_mlx';
    const icon = isMac ? '&#128187;' : '&#128242;';
    const statusColor = w.status === 'active' ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
    const deviceLabel = w.device_model || (isMac ? 'MacBook' : 'iPhone');
    html += `
      <div class="mcp-tool" style="padding:8px 10px;margin-bottom:6px;cursor:default;">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
          <span style="font-size:14px;">${icon}</span>
          <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:600;">${deviceLabel}</span>
          <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);font-family:monospace;">${w.worker_id.slice(0,8)}...</span>
          <span class="status-dot" style="background:${statusColor};width:5px;height:5px;"></span>
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:4px;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;">
          ${w.device_fingerprint ? `<span>FP: ${w.device_fingerprint}</span>` : ''}
          ${w.screen_resolution ? `<span>Screen: ${w.screen_resolution}</span>` : ''}
          ${w.language ? `<span>Lang: ${w.language}</span>` : ''}
          ${w.timezone ? `<span>TZ: ${w.timezone}</span>` : ''}
        </div>
        ${w.webgl_renderer ? `<div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px;">GPU: ${w.webgl_renderer.slice(0,30)}...</div>` : ''}
        ${w.cpu_usage !== null ? `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:4px;">CPU: ${w.cpu_usage.toFixed(1)}% | RAM: ${w.ram_used_mb ? (w.ram_used_mb/w.ram_total_mb*100).toFixed(1) : 0}%</div>` : ''}
      </div>
    `;
  });
  workersList.innerHTML = html;
}

async function loadMCPTools() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/chat/mcp-servers');
    const data = await res.json();
    const tools = data.tools || [];
    mcpToolsEl.innerHTML = '';
    for (const tool of tools) {
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'mcp-tool';
      const isCap = tool.name.includes('_') && !tool.name.startsWith('gbridge_') && !tool.name.startsWith('windsurf_');
      const badgeClass = isCap ? 'badge-cap' : 'badge-mcp';
      const badgeText = isCap ? 'Capability' : 'MCP';
      div.innerHTML = `
        <div class="tool-name">${escapeHtml(tool.name)}</div>
        <div class="tool-desc">${escapeHtml(tool.description || '')}</div>
        <span class="tool-badge ${badgeClass}">${badgeText}</span>
      `;
      div.onclick = () => invokeTool(tool.name, tool.input_schema);
      mcpToolsEl.appendChild(div);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    mcpToolsEl.textContent = 'Failed to load MCP tools';
  }
}

async function invokeTool(name, schema) {
  const args = {};
  const props = schema?.properties || {};
  for (const key of Object.keys(props)) {
    const val = prompt(`Enter ${key}:`, '');
    if (val === null) return;
    args[key] = val;
  }
  sendBtn.disabled = true;
  statusText.textContent = `Running ${name}...`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/chat/mcp-invoke', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({tool_name: name, arguments: args})
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    addMessage('assistant', `Tool result (${name}):\\n` + JSON.stringify(data.result, null, 2));
    statusText.textContent = 'Ready';
  } catch (err) {
    addMessage('assistant', `Tool error: ${err.message}`);
    statusText.textContent = 'Error';
  } finally {
    sendBtn.disabled = false;
  }
}

loadModels();
loadMCPTools();
backendSelect.value = 'hf';
</script>
</body>
</html>"""

from fastapi import Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse
from app.windsurf.packet import generate_windsurf_packet, _list_missing_files, _list_existing_files


def render_windsurf_page(request: Request) -> HTMLResponse:
    existing = _list_existing_files()
    missing = _list_missing_files(existing)
    packet = generate_windsurf_packet()
    return HTMLResponse(content=_windsurf_html(existing, missing, packet), status_code=200)


def _windsurf_html(existing, missing, packet) -> str:
    existing_rows = ''.join([f'<div style="margin-bottom:6px"><span style="color:var(--green)">&#10003;</span> <span style="color:var(--muted);font-family:monospace;font-size:12px">{f}</span></div>' for f in existing[:12]])
    missing_rows = ''.join([f'<div style="margin-bottom:6px"><span style="color:#f87171">&#10007;</span> <span style="color:var(--muted);font-family:monospace;font-size:12px">{f}</span></div>' for f in missing[:8]])
    return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>

<body>
    <div class="container">

                    <input type="email" id="regEmail" placeholder="you@example.com">

                    <div class="value" id="statBalance">$10.00</div>

                    <div class="value" id="statChats">0</div>
                    <div class="label">Chat Sessions</div>
